> DEVELOPER

Implement the following plan:

# Security Hardening: Auth + Shares Fixes

## Context

Two security reviews identified 7 findings. Two are already fixed (M-07 scoped JWT, M-11 Rust Debug redaction). One is documentation-only (M1 plaintext itemName). This plan covers the remaining **4 code fixes**.

## Fixes (in implementation order)

### 1. L1: Always return 200 from `/shares/lookup`

Eliminate 200/404 public key enumeration.

**API (`apps/api/src/shares/shares.controller.ts:149-161`):**
- Remove `throw new NotFoundException(...)` — always return `{ exists }` with 200
- Remove `@ApiResponse({ status: 404, ... })` decorator
- Add response DTO type to `@ApiResponse({ status: 200, type: LookupUserResponseDto })`

**New file: `apps/api/src/shares/dto/lookup-user-response.dto.ts`**
- Class with `@ApiProperty() exists!: boolean` for Orval type generation

**Web (`apps/web/src/services/share.service.ts:71-87`):**
- After `pnpm api:generate`, simplify `lookupUser()` to: `const result = await sharesControllerLookupUser(...); return result.exists;`
- Remove the try/catch 404 pattern

### 2. L4: Add pagination to share listing endpoints

**New file: `apps/api/src/shares/dto/pagination.dto.ts`**
- `PaginationQueryDto` with `limit` (default 50, max 100) and `offset` (default 0), validated with class-validator
- `PaginatedReceivedSharesDto` and `PaginatedSentSharesDto` wrapping `{ shares: [...], total: number }`

**API service (`apps/api/src/shares/shares.service.ts:119-144`):**
- `getReceivedShares(recipientId, limit, offset)` — use `findAndCount` with `take`/`skip`, return `{ shares, total }`
- `getSentShares(sharerId, limit, offset)` — same pattern

**API controller (`apps/api/src/shares/shares.controller.ts:74-134`):**
- Add `@Query() pagination: PaginationQueryDto` to both endpoints
- Update return types to `PaginatedReceivedSharesDto` / `PaginatedSentSharesDto`
- Wrap response in `{ shares: [...mapped], total }`

**Web (`apps/web/src/services/share.service.ts:34-63`):**
- After `pnpm api:generate`, update `fetchReceivedShares` and `fetchSentShares` to accept `limit`/`offset` params and return `{ shares, total }`
- Update `ensureFreshSentShares()` to use high limit (1000) since re-wrapping needs full set
- Update callers in share store / useSharedNavigation to destructure `{ shares }`

### 3. M5: Surface notification when `reWrapForRecipients` fails

No existing toast library — create lightweight Zustand notification store.

**New file: `apps/web/src/stores/notification.store.ts`**
- `Notification` type: `{ id, type: 'info'|'warning'|'error', message, createdAt }`
- Actions: `addNotification`, `dismissNotification`, `clearNotifications`

**New file: `apps/web/src/components/NotificationToast.tsx`**
- Floating bottom-right notifications, auto-dismiss after 8s
- Terminal aesthetic (green/amber border, dark background)

**Wire up:**
- Mount `<NotificationToast />` in `apps/web/src/components/layout/AppShell.tsx`
- Add `useNotificationStore.getState().clearNotifications()` to `apps/web/src/lib/clear-user-stores.ts`

**Update `reWrapForRecipients` (`apps/web/src/services/share.service.ts:258-309`):**
- Return `{ failedRecipients: string[] }` instead of `void`
- Collect failed recipient public keys in array

**Update callers:**
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts` (~line 137, ~line 282): check `failedRecipients.length > 0`, add warning notification
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts` (~line 155): same pattern

### 4. H-08: Encrypt device private key in IndexedDB

**File: `apps/web/src/lib/device/identity.ts`**

Add HKDF-derived AES-256-GCM wrapping (same pattern as `search-index.service.ts:327-348`):

- `deriveDeviceWrappingKey(vaultPrivateKey)` — HKDF with info `"cipherbox-device-key-wrap-v1"`, returns AES-256-GCM CryptoKey
- `saveDeviceKeypair(keypair, vaultPrivateKey)` — encrypt privateKey with AES-GCM, store `{ publicKey, encryptedPrivateKey, iv, version: 2 }`
- `loadDeviceKeypair(vaultPrivateKey)` — if `version !== 2`, return null (legacy migration). Otherwise decrypt and return.
- `getOrCreateDeviceIdentity(vaultPrivateKey)` — pass through to save/load

**Update callers (all 3 files):**
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useAuth.ts:153` — pass `userKeypair.privateKey` (already in scope)
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useDeviceApproval.ts:233,365,387` — get `vaultKeypair.privateKey` from auth store
- `apps/web/src/hooks/useMfa.ts:158` — same pattern

**Migration:** Old plaintext keys lack `version: 2` field → `loadDeviceKeypair` returns null → new keypair generated → auto-registered in device registry (existing behavior). Old device entry becomes orphaned (acceptable per existing design doc).

## Post-implementation

1. Run `pnpm api:generate` after L1 + L4 changes
2. Run `pnpm --filter api test` and `pnpm --filter web test`
3. Run `cd tests/e2e && pnpm exec playwright test tests/share-workflow.spec.ts` if available
4. Document M1 (plaintext itemName) as known limitation in security docs
5. Note M-07 and M-11 as already-fixed in commit message

```
     1→import {
     2→  Injectable,
     3→  NotFoundException,
     4→  ForbiddenException,
     5→  ConflictException,
     6→} from '@nestjs/common';
     7→import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
     8→import { Repository, IsNull, Not } from 'typeorm';
     9→import { Share } from './entities/share.entity';
    10→import { ShareKey } from './entities/share-key.entity';
    11→import { User } from '../auth/entities/user.entity';
    12→import { CreateShareDto } from './dto/create-share.dto';
    13→import { AddShareKeysDto } from './dto/share-key.dto';
    14→
    15→@Injectable()
    16→export class SharesService {
    17→  constructor(
    18→    @InjectRepository(Share)
    19→    private readonly shareRepo: Repository<Share>,
    20→    @InjectRepository(ShareKey)
    21→    private readonly shareKeyRepo: Repository<ShareKey>,
    22→    @InjectRepository(User)
    23→    private readonly userRepo: Repository<User>
    24→  ) {}
    25→
    26→  /**
    27→   * Create a new share record with re-wrapped keys.
    28→   * Validates recipient exists and is not the sharer.
    29→   * Prevents duplicate active shares for the same item/recipient pair.
    30→   */
    31→  async createShare(sharerId: string, dto: CreateShareDto): Promise<Share> {
    32→    // Look up recipient by publicKey
    33→    // Strip 0x prefix if present — DB stores bare hex
    34→    const normalizedPubKey = dto.recipientPublicKey.startsWith('0x')
    35→      ? dto.recipientPublicKey.slice(2)
    36→      : dto.recipientPublicKey;
    37→    const recipient = await this.userRepo.findOne({
    38→      where: { publicKey: normalizedPubKey },
    39→    });
    40→
    41→    if (!recipient) {
    42→      throw new NotFoundException('Recipient not found');
    43→    }
    44→
    45→    if (recipient.id === sharerId) {
    46→      throw new ConflictException('Cannot share with yourself');
    47→    }
    48→
    49→    // Check for existing active share (same sharer, recipient, ipnsName)
    50→    const existing = await this.shareRepo.findOne({
    51→      where: {
    52→        sharerId,
    53→        recipientId: recipient.id,
    54→        ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    55→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
    56→      },
    57→    });
    58→
    59→    if (existing) {
    60→      throw new ConflictException('Share already exists for this item and recipient');
    61→    }
    62→
    63→    // Clean up any revoked-but-not-yet-rotated records for this triple
    64→    // so the new share can be created without unique constraint conflicts
    65→    const revoked = await this.shareRepo.find({
    66→      where: {
    67→        sharerId,
    68→        recipientId: recipient.id,
    69→        ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    70→        revokedAt: Not(IsNull()),
    71→      },
    72→    });
    73→    if (revoked.length > 0) {
    74→      await this.shareRepo.remove(revoked);
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    const share = this.shareRepo.create({
    78→      sharerId,
    79→      recipientId: recipient.id,
    80→      itemType: dto.itemType,
    81→      ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    82→      itemName: dto.itemName,
    83→      encryptedKey: Buffer.from(dto.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
    84→      hiddenByRecipient: false,
    85→      revokedAt: null,
    86→    });
    87→
    88→    let savedShare: typeof share;
    89→    try {
    90→      savedShare = await this.shareRepo.save(share);
    91→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    92→      // Handle race condition: concurrent createShare for the same triple
    93→      if (err instanceof Error && err.message?.includes('duplicate key')) {
    94→        throw new ConflictException('Share already exists for this item and recipient');
    95→      }
    96→      throw err;
    97→    }
    98→
    99→    // Create child keys if provided
   100→    if (dto.childKeys && dto.childKeys.length > 0) {
   101→      const shareKeys = dto.childKeys.map((ck) =>
   102→        this.shareKeyRepo.create({
   103→          shareId: savedShare.id,
   104→          keyType: ck.keyType,
   105→          itemId: ck.itemId,
   106→          encryptedKey: Buffer.from(ck.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   107→        })
   108→      );
   109→      await this.shareKeyRepo.save(shareKeys);
   110→    }
   111→
   112→    return savedShare;
   113→  }
   114→
   115→  /**
   116→   * Get all active, non-hidden shares received by the user.
   117→   * Includes sharer relation for publicKey display.
   118→   */
   119→  async getReceivedShares(recipientId: string): Promise<Share[]> {
   120→    return this.shareRepo.find({
   121→      where: {
   122→        recipientId,
   123→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
   124→        hiddenByRecipient: false,
   125→      },
   126→      relations: ['sharer'],
   127→      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },
   128→    });
   129→  }
   130→
   131→  /**
   132→   * Get all active shares sent by the user.
   133→   * Includes recipient relation for publicKey display.
   134→   */
   135→  async getSentShares(sharerId: string): Promise<Share[]> {
   136→    return this.shareRepo.find({
   137→      where: {
   138→        sharerId,
   139→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
   140→      },
   141→      relations: ['recipient'],
   142→      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },
   143→    });
   144→  }
   145→
   146→  /**
   147→   * Get all re-wrapped child keys for a share.
   148→   * Validates the requesting user is either sharer or recipient.
   149→   */
   150→  async getShareKeys(shareId: string, userId: string): Promise<ShareKey[]> {
   151→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   152→
   153→    if (!share) {
   154→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   155→    }
   156→
   157→    if (share.sharerId !== userId && share.recipientId !== userId) {
   158→      throw new ForbiddenException('Not authorized to access this share');
   159→    }
   160→
   161→    return this.shareKeyRepo.find({
   162→      where: { shareId },
   163→      order: { createdAt: 'ASC' },
   164→    });
   165→  }
   166→
   167→  /**
   168→   * Add or update re-wrapped keys for an existing share.
   169→   * Only the sharer can add keys.
   170→   */
   171→  async addShareKeys(shareId: string, sharerId: string, dto: AddShareKeysDto): Promise<void> {
   172→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   173→
   174→    if (!share) {
   175→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   176→    }
   177→
   178→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   179→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can add keys');
   180→    }
   181→
   182→    // Upsert: insert or update encrypted_key for each itemId
   183→    for (const entry of dto.keys) {
   184→      const existing = await this.shareKeyRepo.findOne({
   185→        where: {
   186→          shareId,
   187→          keyType: entry.keyType,
   188→          itemId: entry.itemId,
   189→        },
   190→      });
   191→
   192→      if (existing) {
   193→        existing.encryptedKey = Buffer.from(entry.encryptedKey, 'hex');
   194→        await this.shareKeyRepo.save(existing);
   195→      } else {
   196→        const shareKey = this.shareKeyRepo.create({
   197→          shareId,
   198→          keyType: entry.keyType,
   199→          itemId: entry.itemId,
   200→          encryptedKey: Buffer.from(entry.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   201→        });
   202→        await this.shareKeyRepo.save(shareKey);
   203→      }
   204→    }
   205→  }
   206→
   207→  /**
   208→   * Soft-delete a share by setting revokedAt.
   209→   * Only the sharer can revoke. ShareKey records are kept for lazy rotation.
   210→   */
   211→  async revokeShare(shareId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {
   212→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   213→
   214→    if (!share) {
   215→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   216→    }
   217→
   218→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   219→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can revoke a share');
   220→    }
   221→
   222→    share.revokedAt = new Date();
   223→    await this.shareRepo.save(share);
   224→  }
   225→
   226→  /**
   227→   * Hide a share from the recipient's view.
   228→   * Only the recipient can hide a share.
   229→   */
   230→  async hideShare(shareId: string, recipientId: string): Promise<void> {
   231→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   232→
   233→    if (!share) {
   234→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   235→    }
   236→
   237→    if (share.recipientId !== recipientId) {
   238→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the recipient can hide a share');
   239→    }
   240→
   241→    share.hiddenByRecipient = true;
   242→    await this.shareRepo.save(share);
   243→  }
   244→
   245→  /**
   246→   * Check if a user with the given secp256k1 public key exists.
   247→   * Used to verify recipient is registered before sharing.
   248→   * Does not expose internal user IDs.
   249→   */
   250→  async lookupUserByPublicKey(publicKey: string): Promise<boolean> {
   251→    // Strip 0x prefix if present — DB stores bare hex
   252→    const normalizedKey = publicKey.startsWith('0x') ? publicKey.slice(2) : publicKey;
   253→    const user = await this.userRepo.findOne({
   254→      where: { publicKey: normalizedKey },
   255→      select: ['id'],
   256→    });
   257→
   258→    return !!user;
   259→  }
   260→
   261→  /**
   262→   * Get shares pending key rotation (revoked but not yet hard-deleted).
   263→   */
   264→  async getPendingRotations(sharerId: string): Promise<Share[]> {
   265→    return this.shareRepo.find({
   266→      where: {
   267→        sharerId,
   268→        revokedAt: Not(IsNull()),
   269→      },
   270→      relations: ['recipient'],
   271→      order: { revokedAt: 'ASC' },
   272→    });
   273→  }
   274→
   275→  /**
   276→   * Hard-delete a share and all associated keys after rotation is complete.
   277→   * Only the sharer can complete the rotation.
   278→   */
   279→  async completeRotation(shareId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {
   280→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   281→
   282→    if (!share) {
   283→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   284→    }
   285→
   286→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   287→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can complete rotation');
   288→    }
   289→
   290→    if (!share.revokedAt) {
   291→      throw new ConflictException('Cannot complete rotation for a non-revoked share');
   292→    }
   293→
   294→    // CASCADE will remove all associated ShareKey records
   295→    await this.shareRepo.remove(share);
   296→  }
   297→
   298→  /**
   299→   * Update the encrypted key on an existing share.
   300→   * Used after lazy key rotation to re-wrap the new folder key for remaining recipients.
   301→   */
   302→  async updateShareEncryptedKey(
   303→    shareId: string,
   304→    sharerId: string,
   305→    encryptedKey: string
   306→  ): Promise<void> {
   307→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   308→
   309→    if (!share) {
   310→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   311→    }
   312→
   313→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   314→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can update share keys');
   315→    }
   316→
   317→    share.encryptedKey = Buffer.from(encryptedKey, 'hex');
   318→    await this.shareRepo.save(share);
   319→  }
   320→}
   321→
```

```
     1→/**
     2→ * Share Service - API integration for user-to-user sharing
     3→ *
     4→ * Wraps the generated Orval API client for share endpoints.
     5→ * All sharing operations flow through these functions.
     6→ *
     7→ * Security: The server never sees plaintext keys. All keys are
     8→ * ECIES-wrapped for the recipient before being sent to the API.
     9→ */
    10→
    11→import {
    12→  sharesControllerCreateShare,
    13→  sharesControllerGetReceivedShares,
    14→  sharesControllerGetSentShares,
    15→  sharesControllerLookupUser,
    16→  sharesControllerGetShareKeys,

    30→/**
    31→ * Fetch all active, non-hidden shares received by the current user.
    32→ * Updates the share store with the results.

    34→export async function fetchReceivedShares(): Promise<ReceivedShare[]> {
    35→  const response = await sharesControllerGetReceivedShares();
    36→
    37→  return response.map((s) => ({

    45→  }));
    46→}
    47→
    48→/**
    49→ * Fetch all active shares sent by the current user.
    50→ * Updates the share store with the results.
    51→ */
    52→export async function fetchSentShares(): Promise<SentShare[]> {
    53→  const response = await sharesControllerGetSentShares();
    54→
    55→  return response.map((s) => ({
    56→    shareId: s.shareId,
    57→    recipientPublicKey: s.recipientPublicKey,
    58→    itemType: s.itemType as 'folder' | 'file',
    59→    ipnsName: s.ipnsName,
    60→    itemName: s.itemName,
    61→    createdAt: String(s.createdAt),

    64→
    65→/**
    66→ * Check if a CipherBox user exists with the given secp256k1 public key.
    67→ * Returns false if no user is found (404).
    68→ *
    69→ * @param publicKeyHex - Uncompressed secp256k1 public key (0x04... format)
    70→ */
    71→export async function lookupUser(publicKeyHex: string): Promise<boolean> {
    72→  try {
    73→    await sharesControllerLookupUser({ publicKey: publicKeyHex });
    74→    return true;
    75→  } catch (err: unknown) {
    76→    // Only treat 404 as "user not found"; re-throw network/server errors
    77→    if (
    78→      err &&
    79→      typeof err === 'object' &&
    80→      'status' in err &&
    81→      (err as { status: number }).status === 404
    82→    ) {
    83→      return false;
    84→    }
    85→    throw err;
    86→  }
    87→}
    88→
    89→/**
    90→ * Create a new share, sharing an encrypted folder or file with another user.
    91→ *
    92→ * @param params.recipientPublicKey - Recipient's secp256k1 public key
    93→ * @param params.itemType - 'folder' or 'file'
    94→ * @param params.ipnsName - IPNS name of the shared item
    95→ * @param params.itemName - Display name of the shared item
    96→ * @param params.encryptedKey - Hex-encoded ECIES ciphertext of the item key
    97→ * @param params.childKeys - Optional re-wrapped descendant keys
    98→ */
    99→export async function createShare(params: {
   100→  recipientPublicKey: string;
   101→  itemType: 'folder' | 'file';
   102→  ipnsName: string;
   103→  itemName: string;
   104→  encryptedKey: string;
   105→  childKeys?: Array<{ keyType: 'file' | 'folder'; itemId: string; encryptedKey: string }>;
   106→}): Promise<{ shareId: string }> {
   107→  const response = await sharesControllerCreateShare({

   109→    itemType: params.itemType,
   110→    ipnsName: params.ipnsName,

   112→    encryptedKey: params.encryptedKey,
   113→    childKeys: params.childKeys?.map((k) => ({
   114→      keyType: k.keyType,
   115→      itemId: k.itemId,
   116→      encryptedKey: k.encryptedKey,
   117→    })),
   118→  });
   119→
   120→  return { shareId: response.shareId };
   121→}
   122→
   123→/**
   124→ * Revoke a share (soft-delete). Only the sharer can revoke.
   125→ * Keys are kept for lazy rotation.

   127→export async function revokeShare(shareId: string): Promise<void> {
   128→  await sharesControllerRevokeShare(shareId);
   129→}
   130→
   131→/**
   132→ * Hide a share from the recipient's view. Only the recipient can hide.
   133→ */
   134→export async function hideShare(shareId: string): Promise<void> {
   135→  await sharesControllerHideShare(shareId);
   136→}
   137→
   138→/**
   139→ * Get all re-wrapped child keys for a share.
   140→ * Accessible by both sharer and recipient.
   141→ */
   142→export async function fetchShareKeys(
   143→  shareId: string
   144→): Promise<Array<{ keyType: 'file' | 'folder'; itemId: string; encryptedKey: string }>> {
   145→  const response = await sharesControllerGetShareKeys(shareId);
   146→
   147→  return response.map((k) => ({
   148→    keyType: k.keyType as 'file' | 'folder',
   149→    itemId: k.itemId,
   150→    encryptedKey: k.encryptedKey,
   151→  }));
   152→}
   153→
   154→/**
   155→ * Add re-wrapped child keys to an existing share. Only the sharer can add keys.
   156→ */
   157→export async function addShareKeys(
   158→  shareId: string,
   159→  keys: Array<{ keyType: 'file' | 'folder'; itemId: string; encryptedKey: string }>
   160→): Promise<void> {
   161→  await sharesControllerAddShareKeys(shareId, {
   162→    keys: keys.map((k) => ({
   163→      keyType: k.keyType,
   164→      itemId: k.itemId,
   165→      encryptedKey: k.encryptedKey,
   166→    })),
   167→  });
   168→}
   169→
   170→/**
   171→ * Get sent shares for a specific item (by IPNS name).
   172→ * Fetches all sent shares and filters by ipnsName.
   173→ * Uses the store cache if available and fresh.
   174→ */
   175→export async function getSentSharesForItem(ipnsName: string): Promise<SentShare[]> {
   176→  const shares = await ensureFreshSentShares();
   177→  return shares.filter((s) => s.ipnsName === ipnsName);
   178→}
   179→
   180→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181→// Post-upload / post-create share key propagation
   182→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183→
   184→/**
   185→ * Ensure sent shares cache is fresh (fetched within last 30s).
   186→ * Returns the current sent shares array.
   187→ */
   188→async function ensureFreshSentShares(): Promise<SentShare[]> {
   189→  const store = useShareStore.getState();
   190→  if (store.lastSentFetchedAt && Date.now() - store.lastSentFetchedAt < 30_000) {
   191→    return store.sentShares;
   192→  }
   193→  const shares = await fetchSentShares();
   194→  useShareStore.getState().setSentShares(shares);
   195→  return shares;
   196→}
   197→
   198→/**
   199→ * Check if a folder (by IPNS name) has any active shares.
   200→ * Used to decide whether post-upload re-wrapping is needed.
   201→ */
   202→export async function hasActiveShares(folderIpnsName: string): Promise<boolean> {
   203→  const shares = await ensureFreshSentShares();
   204→  return shares.some((s) => s.ipnsName === folderIpnsName);
   205→}
   206→
   207→/**
   208→ * Find active shares that cover a given folder, including ancestor shares.
   209→ * A folder is "covered" if it or any of its ancestor folders is shared.
   210→ *
   211→ * Walks the ancestor chain and checks each folder's IPNS name against sent shares.
   212→ *
   213→ * @param folderIpnsName - IPNS name of the current folder
   214→ * @param folders - Current folder tree from the folder store
   215→ * @param currentFolderId - ID of the current folder in the tree
   216→ * @returns Array of sent shares covering this folder (may be from ancestor)
   217→ */
   218→export async function findCoveringShares(
   219→  folderIpnsName: string,
   220→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>,
   221→  currentFolderId: string | null
   222→): Promise<SentShare[]> {
   223→  const shares = await ensureFreshSentShares();
   224→  if (shares.length === 0) return [];
   225→
   226→  // Collect all IPNS names from this folder up to root
   227→  const ipnsNames = new Set<string>();
   228→  ipnsNames.add(folderIpnsName);
   229→
   230→  let walkId = currentFolderId;
   231→  while (walkId) {
   232→    const node = folders[walkId];
   233→    if (!node) break;
   234→    ipnsNames.add(node.ipnsName);
   235→    walkId = node.parentId;
   236→  }
   237→
   238→  return shares.filter((s) => ipnsNames.has(s.ipnsName));
   239→}
   240→
   241→/**
   242→ * After adding a file or subfolder to a shared folder, re-wrap the new key
   243→ * for all existing share recipients.
   244→ *
   245→ * This is a fire-and-forget operation -- failures are logged but don't block
   246→ * the primary upload/create flow.
   247→ *
   248→ * IMPORTANT: Callers are responsible for zeroing `newItems[*].plaintextKey`
   249→ * after this function completes. This function does NOT zero the keys because
   250→ * some callers (e.g., subfolder creation) keep the key alive in the store.
   251→ * Use a `finally` block to ensure zeroing even on errors.
   252→ *
   253→ * @param params.folderIpnsName - IPNS name of the folder being modified
   254→ * @param params.folders - Current folder tree from the store
   255→ * @param params.currentFolderId - ID of the current folder in the tree
   256→ * @param params.newItems - New items whose keys need re-wrapping
   257→ */
   258→export async function reWrapForRecipients(params: {
   259→  folderIpnsName: string;
   260→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>;
   261→  currentFolderId: string | null;
   262→  newItems: Array<{
   263→    keyType: 'file' | 'folder';
   264→    itemId: string;
   265→    plaintextKey: Uint8Array;
   266→  }>;
   267→}): Promise<void> {
   268→  const coveringShares = await findCoveringShares(
   269→    params.folderIpnsName,
   270→    params.folders,
   271→    params.currentFolderId
   272→  );
   273→
   274→  if (coveringShares.length === 0) return;
   275→
   276→  // For each share recipient, re-wrap all new item keys
   277→  for (const share of coveringShares) {
   278→    try {
   279→      const recipientPubKey = hexToBytes(
   280→        share.recipientPublicKey.startsWith('0x')
   281→          ? share.recipientPublicKey.slice(2)
   282→          : share.recipientPublicKey
   283→      );
   284→
   285→      const wrappedKeys: Array<{
   286→        keyType: 'file' | 'folder';
   287→        itemId: string;
   288→        encryptedKey: string;
   289→      }> = [];
   290→
   291→      for (const item of params.newItems) {
   292→        const wrapped = await wrapKey(item.plaintextKey, recipientPubKey);
   293→        wrappedKeys.push({
   294→          keyType: item.keyType,
   295→          itemId: item.itemId,
   296→          encryptedKey: bytesToHex(wrapped),
   297→        });
   298→      }
   299→
   300→      // Add the wrapped keys to this share via API
   301→      await addShareKeys(share.shareId, wrappedKeys);
   302→    } catch (err) {
   303→      console.warn(
   304→        `[share] Failed to re-wrap keys for recipient ${share.recipientPublicKey.slice(0, 10)}...:`,
   305→        err
   306→      );
   307→    }
   308→  }
   309→}
   310→
   311→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   312→// Lazy key rotation after revocation
   313→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   314→
   315→/** A revoked share pending key rotation. */
   316→export type PendingRotation = {
   317→  shareId: string;
   318→  recipientPublicKey: string;
   319→  itemType: 'folder' | 'file';
   320→  ipnsName: string;
   321→  itemName: string;
   322→  revokedAt: string;
   323→};
   324→
   325→/**
   326→ * Fetch revoked shares that are pending key rotation from the server.
   327→ * These are shares where revokedAt is set but the share has not been hard-deleted.
   328→ */
   329→export async function fetchPendingRotations(): Promise<PendingRotation[]> {
   330→  const response = await sharesControllerGetPendingRotations();
   331→
   332→  return response.map((r) => ({
   333→    shareId: r.shareId,
   334→    recipientPublicKey: r.recipientPublicKey,
   335→    itemType: r.itemType as 'folder' | 'file',
   336→    ipnsName: r.ipnsName,
   337→    itemName: r.itemName,
   338→    revokedAt: String(r.revokedAt),
   339→  }));
   340→}
   341→
   342→/**
   343→ * Check if a folder has pending rotations (revoked shares awaiting key rotation).
   344→ * Called before any folder modification.
   345→ *
   346→ * @param folderIpnsName - IPNS name of the folder being modified
   347→ * @returns true if there are revoked shares for this folder that need rotation
   348→ */
   349→export async function checkPendingRotation(folderIpnsName: string): Promise<boolean> {
   350→  const pendingRotations = await fetchPendingRotations();
   351→  return pendingRotations.some((r) => r.ipnsName === folderIpnsName);
   352→}
   353→
   354→/**
   355→ * Update the encrypted key on a share record after lazy key rotation.
   356→ * Re-wraps the new folder key for a remaining (non-revoked) recipient.
   357→ */
   358→export async function updateShareKey(shareId: string, encryptedKey: string): Promise<void> {
   359→  await sharesControllerUpdateShareEncryptedKey(shareId, { encryptedKey });
   360→}
   361→
   362→/**
   363→ * Hard-delete a revoked share after rotation is complete.
   364→ */
   365→export async function completeShareRotation(shareId: string): Promise<void> {
   366→  await sharesControllerCompleteRotation(shareId);
   367→}
   368→
   369→/**
   370→ * Execute lazy key rotation for a folder.
   371→ * Called when a folder modification is about to happen and pending rotations exist.
   372→ *
   373→ * Protocol:
   374→ * 1. Generate new random folderKey
   375→ * 2. Re-wrap new folderKey for each REMAINING (non-revoked) active recipient
   376→ * 3. Update remaining shares with the new encrypted key
   377→ * 4. Hard-delete revoked share records (rotation complete)
   378→ * 5. Invalidate share cache
   379→ *
   380→ * NOTE: The actual folder metadata re-encryption (decrypt with old key, re-encrypt
   381→ * with new key, re-publish IPNS) is handled by the caller (folder.service.ts)
   382→ * since it has access to the folder's IPNS private key and publishing infrastructure.
   383→ *
   384→ * @returns The new folderKey for the caller to use
   385→ */
   386→export async function executeLazyRotation(params: {
   387→  folderIpnsName: string;
   388→  oldFolderKey: Uint8Array;
   389→  ownerPublicKey: Uint8Array;
   390→}): Promise<{ newFolderKey: Uint8Array }> {
   391→  // 1. Generate new random 32-byte folderKey
   392→  const newFolderKey = generateRandomBytes(32);
   393→
   394→  // 2. Fetch pending rotations and active shares for this folder
   395→  const [pendingRotations, activeSentShares] = await Promise.all([
   396→    fetchPendingRotations(),
   397→    getSentSharesForItem(params.folderIpnsName),
   398→  ]);
   399→
   400→  const revokedForFolder = pendingRotations.filter((r) => r.ipnsName === params.folderIpnsName);
   401→  const revokedShareIds = new Set(revokedForFolder.map((r) => r.shareId));
   402→
   403→  // Active shares that are NOT revoked -- these recipients keep access
   404→  const remainingShares = activeSentShares.filter((s) => !revokedShareIds.has(s.shareId));
   405→
   406→  // 3. Re-wrap new folderKey for each remaining recipient
   407→  const reWrapFailures: string[] = [];
   408→  for (const share of remainingShares) {
   409→    try {
   410→      const recipientPubKey = hexToBytes(
   411→        share.recipientPublicKey.startsWith('0x')
   412→          ? share.recipientPublicKey.slice(2)
   413→          : share.recipientPublicKey
   414→      );
   415→      const wrapped = await wrapKey(newFolderKey, recipientPubKey);
   416→      await updateShareKey(share.shareId, bytesToHex(wrapped));
   417→    } catch (err) {
   418→      console.warn(
   419→        `[share] Failed to update share key for remaining recipient ${share.recipientPublicKey.slice(0, 10)}...:`,
   420→        err
   421→      );
   422→      reWrapFailures.push(share.shareId);
   423→    }
   424→  }
   425→
   426→  if (reWrapFailures.length > 0) {
   427→    newFolderKey.fill(0);
   428→    throw new Error(
   429→      `Key rotation failed: could not re-wrap key for ${reWrapFailures.length} recipient(s). ` +
   430→        'Aborting to prevent inconsistent state.'
   431→    );
   432→  }
   433→
   434→  // 4. Hard-delete all revoked shares for this folder
   435→  for (const revoked of revokedForFolder) {
   436→    try {
   437→      await completeShareRotation(revoked.shareId);
   438→    } catch (err) {
   439→      console.warn(`[share] Failed to complete rotation for share ${revoked.shareId}:`, err);
   440→    }
   441→  }
   442→
   443→  // 5. Invalidate the sent shares cache so next check fetches fresh state
   444→  useShareStore.getState().setSentShares([]);
   445→
   446→  return { newFolderKey };
   447→}
   448→
```

```
     1→/**
     2→ * Device Identity Persistence
     3→ *
     4→ * Manages the device's Ed25519 keypair in IndexedDB. This keypair is unique
     5→ * per physical device/browser and cannot be re-derived (unlike the user's
     6→ * vault key which comes from Web3Auth).
     7→ *
     8→ * If IndexedDB is unavailable (incognito, etc.) or the stored keypair is
     9→ * cleared by the browser, a new keypair is generated. This creates a new
    10→ * device entry in the registry (the old one becomes orphaned).
    11→ */
    12→
    13→import { generateDeviceKeypair, deriveDeviceId, type DeviceKeypair } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    14→
    15→const DB_NAME = 'cipherbox-device';
    16→const DB_VERSION = 1;
    17→const STORE_NAME = 'keys';
    18→const KEYPAIR_KEY = 'device-ed25519';
    19→
    20→/**
    21→ * Open the IndexedDB database, creating the object store on upgrade.
    22→ */
    23→function openDB(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
    24→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    25→    const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);
    26→    request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
    27→      request.result.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME);
    28→    };
    29→    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
    30→    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
    31→  });
    32→}
    33→
    34→/**
    35→ * Load device keypair from IndexedDB.
    36→ *
    37→ * Stores keys as plain number arrays because Uint8Array doesn't
    38→ * serialize reliably across all browsers in IndexedDB.
    39→ *
    40→ * @returns Keypair if found, null if not found or IndexedDB unavailable
    41→ */
    42→async function loadDeviceKeypair(): Promise<{
    43→  publicKey: Uint8Array;
    44→  privateKey: Uint8Array;
    45→} | null> {
    46→  try {
    47→    const db = await openDB();
    48→    const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readonly');
    49→    const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    50→
    51→    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    52→      const request = store.get(KEYPAIR_KEY);
    53→      request.onsuccess = () => {
    54→        const val = request.result;
    55→        if (val && val.publicKey && val.privateKey) {
    56→          resolve({
    57→            publicKey: new Uint8Array(val.publicKey),
    58→            privateKey: new Uint8Array(val.privateKey),
    59→          });
    60→        } else {
    61→          resolve(null);
    62→        }
    63→      };
    64→      request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
    65→    });
    66→  } catch {
    67→    // IndexedDB unavailable (incognito, etc.)
    68→    return null;
    69→  }
    70→}
    71→
    72→/**
    73→ * Save device keypair to IndexedDB.
    74→ *
    75→ * Converts Uint8Array to plain number arrays before storing
    76→ * to avoid serialization issues across browsers.
    77→ */
    78→async function saveDeviceKeypair(keypair: {
    79→  publicKey: Uint8Array;
    80→  privateKey: Uint8Array;
    81→}): Promise<void> {
    82→  const db = await openDB();
    83→  const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite');
    84→  const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    85→
    86→  store.put(
    87→    {
    88→      publicKey: Array.from(keypair.publicKey),
    89→      privateKey: Array.from(keypair.privateKey),
    90→    },
    91→    KEYPAIR_KEY
    92→  );
    93→
    94→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    95→    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    96→    tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
    97→  });
    98→}
    99→
   100→/**
   101→ * Get or create the device's Ed25519 identity.
   102→ *
   103→ * Tries to load an existing keypair from IndexedDB first.
   104→ * If not found, generates a new one and persists it.
   105→ *
   106→ * The returned DeviceKeypair includes:
   107→ * - publicKey: 32-byte Ed25519 public key
   108→ * - privateKey: 32-byte Ed25519 private key (seed)
   109→ * - deviceId: SHA-256 hex of the public key
   110→ *
   111→ * @returns Device keypair with derived device ID
   112→ */
   113→export async function getOrCreateDeviceIdentity(): Promise<DeviceKeypair> {
   114→  // Try loading existing keypair from IndexedDB
   115→  const stored = await loadDeviceKeypair();
   116→  if (stored) {
   117→    const deviceId = deriveDeviceId(stored.publicKey);
   118→    return {
   119→      publicKey: stored.publicKey,
   120→      privateKey: stored.privateKey,
   121→      deviceId,
   122→    };
   123→  }
   124→
   125→  // Generate new keypair and persist
   126→  const keypair = generateDeviceKeypair();
   127→  await saveDeviceKeypair({
   128→    publicKey: keypair.publicKey,
   129→    privateKey: keypair.privateKey,
   130→  });
   131→
   132→  return keypair;
   133→}
   134→
```

```
   329→
   330→  /**

   347→            idToken: cipherboxJwt,
   348→            publicKey: `pending-core-kit-${userId}`,
   349→            loginType: 'corekit',
   350→          });
   351→          setAccessToken(tempLoginResponse.accessToken);
   352→          setUserEmail(email);
   353→          return;
   354→        }
   355→
   356→        // Normal path
   357→        await completeBackendAuth('email', cipherboxJwt);
   358→        setUserEmail(email);
   359→        // Navigation handled by Login.tsx redirect effect (isAuthenticated → /files)
   360→      } catch (error) {
   361→        console.error('[useAuth] Email login failed:', error);
   362→        throw error;
   363→      } finally {
   364→        setIsLoggingIn(false);
   365→      }
   366→    },
   367→    [isLoggingIn, coreKitLoginEmail, completeBackendAuth, setAccessToken, setUserEmail]
   368→  );
   369→
   370→  /**
   371→   * Login with Wallet (SIWE).
   372→   * Flow: Wallet connects + signs SIWE message -> backend verifies ->
   373→   * CipherBox JWT -> Core Kit loginWithJWT -> backend /auth/login (corekit type)
   374→   */
   375→  const loginWithWallet = useCallback(
   376→    async (cipherboxJwt: string, userId: string): Promise<void> => {
   377→      if (isLoggingIn) return;
   378→      setIsLoggingIn(true);
   379→      try {
   380→        // 1. Core Kit login via CipherBox identity provider
   381→        const { status: coreKitStatus } = await coreKitLoginWallet(cipherboxJwt, userId);
   382→
   383→        if (coreKitStatus === 'required_share') {
   384→          setPendingAuth(cipherboxJwt, 'wallet');
   385→
   386→          const tempLoginResponse = await authApi.login({
   387→            idToken: cipherboxJwt,
   388→            publicKey: `pending-core-kit-${userId}`,
   389→            loginType: 'corekit',
   390→          });
   391→          setAccessToken(tempLoginResponse.accessToken);
   392→          return;
   393→        }
   394→
   395→        // Normal path
   396→        await completeBackendAuth('wallet', cipherboxJwt);
   397→        // Navigation is handled by Login.tsx's redirect effect (isAuthenticated → /files).
   398→        // completeBackendAuth sets the access token early, but initializeOrLoadVault()
   399→        // inside it may still be pending. An explicit navigate here would fire AFTER
   400→        // vault init completes — by which point the user may have navigated away from
   401→        // the login page, causing a disruptive late redirect.
   402→      } catch (error) {
   403→        console.error('[useAuth] Wallet login failed:', error);
   404→        throw error;
   405→      } finally {
   406→        setIsLoggingIn(false);
   407→      }
   408→    },
   409→    [isLoggingIn, coreKitLoginWallet, completeBackendAuth, setAccessToken]
   410→  );
   411→
   412→  /**
   413→   * Legacy login() for backward compatibility with AuthButton.
   414→   * With Core Kit, login requires specifying the auth method.
   415→   * This will be replaced by the new Login UI in Plan 04.
   416→   */
   417→  const login = useCallback(async () => {
   418→    // Plan 04 will build the new login UI with method selection.
   419→    // For now, this is a no-op placeholder that AuthButton calls.
   420→    console.warn(
   421→      '[useAuth] login() called without method -- ' +
   422→        'use loginWithGoogle() or loginWithEmail() instead. ' +
   423→        'Plan 04 will provide the new Login UI.'
   424→    );
   425→  }, []);
   426→
   427→  // Complete logout: Backend -> Core Kit -> Clear state
   428→  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
   429→    if (isLoggingOut) return;
   430→
   431→    setIsLoggingOut(true);
   432→    try {
   433→      // 1. Call backend logout (clears cookie)
   434→      if (accessToken) {
   435→        try {
   436→          await authApi.logout();
   437→        } catch {
   438→          // Ignore errors -- we'll clear state anyway
   439→        }
   440→      }
   441→
   442→      // 2. Logout Core Kit (clears session from localStorage)
   443→      await coreKitLogout();
   444→
   445→      // 3. Clear all user-scoped stores (centralized helper)
   446→      clearAllUserStores();
   447→
   448→      // 4. Navigate to login
   449→      navigate('/');
   450→    } catch (error) {
   451→      console.error('[useAuth] Logout failed:', error);
   452→      // Still clear state even if backend fails
   453→      clearAllUserStores();
   454→      navigate('/');
   455→    } finally {
   456→      setIsLoggingOut(false);
   457→    }
   458→  }, [accessToken, coreKitLogout, navigate, isLoggingOut]);
   459→
   460→  // Keep function refs up-to-date for use in session restoration effect.
   461→  // Using refs prevents the effect from re-firing when function identities change.
   462→  initializeOrLoadVaultRef.current = initializeOrLoadVault;
   463→  coreKitLogoutRef.current = coreKitLogout;
   464→
   465→  // Session restoration: if Core Kit restores a session from localStorage
   466→  // on init, we have LOGGED_IN status without going through login flow.
   467→  // Complete backend auth + vault loading.
   468→  useEffect(() => {
   469→    const restoreSession = async () => {
   470→      // Only restore if Core Kit has a session but we don't have
   471→      // a backend access token yet
   472→      if (coreKitLoggedIn && !isAuthenticated && !isLoggingIn && !restoringRef.current) {
   473→        restoringRef.current = true;
   474→        setIsLoggingIn(true);
   475→        try {
   476→          // Try to refresh using the HTTP-only cookie first
   477→          const response = await authApi.refresh();
   478→          setAccessToken(response.accessToken);
   479→
   480→          // Restore email from backend if available
   481→          if (response.email) {
   482→            setUserEmail(response.email);
   483→          }
   484→
   485→          // Load vault keys from Core Kit keypair
   486→          await initializeOrLoadVaultRef.current?.();
   487→        } catch {
   488→          // No valid backend session -- user needs to re-login
   489→          // Core Kit session exists but backend cookie expired
   490→          // Clear Core Kit session to avoid inconsistent state
   491→          try {
   492→            await coreKitLogoutRef.current?.();
   493→          } catch {
   494→            // Ignore logout errors during cleanup
   495→          }
   496→        } finally {
   497→          restoringRef.current = false;
   498→          setIsLoggingIn(false);
   499→        }
   500→      }
   501→    };
   502→    restoreSession();
   503→  }, [coreKitLoggedIn, isAuthenticated, isLoggingIn, setAccessToken, setUserEmail]);
   504→
   505→  return {
   506→    isLoading,
   507→    isAuthenticated,
   508→    isRequiredShare,
   509→    lastAuthMethod,
   510→    userEmail,
   511→    pendingAuthMethod,
   512→    login,
   513→    loginWithGoogle,
   514→    loginWithEmail,
   515→    loginWithWallet,
   516→    completeRequiredShare,
   517→    logout,
   518→  };
   519→}
   520→
```

```
     1→/**
     2→ * useDeviceApproval Hook
     3→ *
     4→ * Manages the full lifecycle for BOTH sides of the cross-device approval flow:
     5→ *
     6→ * **New device (requester):**
     7→ * - Generate ephemeral secp256k1 keypair
     8→ * - Create bulletin board request
     9→ * - Poll for approval status
    10→ * - On approval: ECIES-decrypt factor key, inputFactorKey, create device factor
    11→ *
    12→ * **Existing device (approver):**
    13→ * - Poll for pending approval requests
    14→ * - Approve: ECIES-encrypt current factor key with requester's ephemeral public key
    15→ * - Deny: mark request as denied
    16→ */
    17→
    18→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
    19→import * as secp256k1 from '@noble/secp256k1';
    20→import {
    21→  generateFactorKey,
    22→  TssShareType,
    23→  FactorKeyTypeShareDescription,
    24→} from '@web3auth/mpc-core-kit';
    25→import { wrapKey, unwrapKey, hexToBytes, bytesToHex } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    26→import { useCoreKit } from '../lib/web3auth/core-kit-provider';
    27→import { useMfa } from './useMfa';
    28→import { useVisibility } from './useVisibility';
    29→import { useAuth } from './useAuth';
    30→import { getOrCreateDeviceIdentity } from '../lib/device/identity';
    31→import { detectDeviceInfo } from '../lib/device/info';
    32→import {
    33→  deviceApprovalApi,
    34→  type ApprovalStatusResponse,
    35→  type PendingApproval,
    36→} from '../services/device-approval.service';
    37→
    38→export type ApprovalStatus =
    39→  | 'idle'
    40→  | 'requesting'
    41→  | 'pending'
    42→  | 'approved'
    43→  | 'denied'
    44→  | 'expired'
    45→  | 'completing'
    46→  | 'error';
    47→
    48→export function useDeviceApproval() {
    49→  const { coreKit } = useCoreKit();
    50→  const { inputFactorKey } = useMfa();
    51→  const { completeRequiredShare } = useAuth();
    52→  const isVisible = useVisibility();
    53→
    54→  // --- New device (requester) state ---
    55→  const [approvalStatus, setApprovalStatus] = useState<ApprovalStatus>('idle');
    56→  const [approvalError, setApprovalError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    57→  const requestIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    58→  const ephemeralPrivKeyRef = useRef<Uint8Array | null>(null);
    59→  // Generation counter to detect stale requestApproval completions.
    60→  // In React StrictMode, the effect fires twice (mount→unmount→remount), launching
    61→  // two concurrent requestApproval() calls whose async completions can race.
    62→  // Each call increments the counter; after each await, the call checks if it's
    63→  // still the active generation — if not, it cancels itself and returns early.
    64→  const requestGenRef = useRef(0);
    65→  const requesterPollRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
    66→
    67→  // --- Existing device (approver) state ---
    68→  const [pendingRequests, setPendingRequests] = useState<PendingApproval[]>([]);
    69→  const isPollingPendingRef = useRef(false);
    70→  const approverPollRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
    71→  // Track recently approved/denied request IDs to filter out stale polling responses.
    72→  // Without this, the 5s polling interval can re-add a request that was just handled
    73→  // if the poll response was already in-flight when the respond API call completed.
    74→  const handledRequestIdsRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
    75→
    76→  /**
    77→   * Zero-fill and clear ephemeral private key from memory.
    78→   */
    79→  const clearEphemeralKey = useCallback(() => {
    80→    if (ephemeralPrivKeyRef.current) {
    81→      ephemeralPrivKeyRef.current.fill(0);
    82→      ephemeralPrivKeyRef.current = null;
    83→    }
    84→  }, []);
    85→
    86→  /**
    87→   * Stop requester polling.
    88→   */
    89→  const stopRequesterPolling = useCallback(() => {
    90→    if (requesterPollRef.current) {
    91→      clearInterval(requesterPollRef.current);
    92→      requesterPollRef.current = null;
    93→    }
    94→  }, []);
    95→
    96→  /**
    97→   * Stop approver polling.
    98→   */
    99→  const stopApproverPolling = useCallback(() => {
   100→    if (approverPollRef.current) {
   101→      clearInterval(approverPollRef.current);
   102→      approverPollRef.current = null;
   103→    }
   104→    isPollingPendingRef.current = false;
   105→    handledRequestIdsRef.current.clear();
   106→  }, []);
   107→
   108→  // =========================================================================
   109→  // NEW DEVICE SIDE (REQUESTER)
   110→  // =========================================================================
   111→
   112→  /**
   113→   * Handle a successful approval: decrypt factor key, input it, create device factor.
   114→   */
   115→  const handleApprovalSuccess = useCallback(
   116→    async (encryptedFactorKeyHex: string) => {
   117→      setApprovalStatus('completing');
   118→
   119→      try {
   120→        const ephPrivKey = ephemeralPrivKeyRef.current;
   121→        if (!ephPrivKey) {
   122→          throw new Error('Ephemeral private key not available');
   123→        }
   124→        if (!coreKit) {
   125→          throw new Error('Core Kit not initialized');
   126→        }
   127→
   128→        // 1. ECIES-decrypt the factor key
   129→        const encrypted = hexToBytes(encryptedFactorKeyHex);
   130→        const factorKeyBytes = await unwrapKey(encrypted, ephPrivKey);
   131→        const factorKeyHex = bytesToHex(factorKeyBytes);
   132→        factorKeyBytes.fill(0); // Zero-fill decrypted key material after conversion
   133→
   134→        // 2. Input the factor key to complete Core Kit login
   135→        await inputFactorKey(factorKeyHex);
   136→
   137→        // 3. Create device factor for this new device
   138→        const newDeviceFactor = generateFactorKey();
   139→        await coreKit.createFactor({
   140→          shareType: TssShareType.DEVICE,
   141→          factorKey: newDeviceFactor.private,
   142→          shareDescription: FactorKeyTypeShareDescription.DeviceShare,
   143→        });
   144→        await coreKit.setDeviceFactor(newDeviceFactor.private);
   145→        await coreKit.commitChanges();
   146→
   147→        // 4. Clean up ephemeral key
   148→        clearEphemeralKey();
   149→
   150→        // 5. Complete the login flow
   151→        await completeRequiredShare();
   152→
   153→        setApprovalStatus('approved');
   154→      } catch (err) {
   155→        stopRequesterPolling();
   156→        clearEphemeralKey();
   157→        setApprovalError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to complete approval');
   158→        setApprovalStatus('error');
   159→      }
   160→    },
   161→    [coreKit, inputFactorKey, completeRequiredShare, clearEphemeralKey, stopRequesterPolling]
   162→  );
   163→
   164→  /**
   165→   * Start polling for approval status (new device side).
   166→   */
   167→  const startRequesterPolling = useCallback(
   168→    (requestId: string) => {
   169→      stopRequesterPolling();
   170→
   171→      const poll = async () => {
   172→        // Separate network fetch from success handling so network errors
   173→        // don't swallow failures from handleApprovalSuccess/completeRequiredShare
   174→        let result: ApprovalStatusResponse;
   175→        try {
   176→          result = await deviceApprovalApi.getStatus(requestId);
   177→        } catch {
   178→          // Network error -- continue polling, don't crash
   179→          return;
   180→        }
   181→
   182→        if (result.status === 'approved' && result.encryptedFactorKey) {
   183→          stopRequesterPolling();
   184→          await handleApprovalSuccess(result.encryptedFactorKey);
   185→        } else if (result.status === 'denied') {
   186→          stopRequesterPolling();
   187→          clearEphemeralKey();
   188→          setApprovalStatus('denied');
   189→        } else if (result.status === 'expired') {
   190→          stopRequesterPolling();
   191→          clearEphemeralKey();
   192→          setApprovalStatus('expired');
   193→        }
   194→        // 'pending' -> continue polling
   195→      };
   196→
   197→      requesterPollRef.current = setInterval(poll, 3000);
   198→      // Fire immediately
   199→      void poll();
   200→    },
   201→    [stopRequesterPolling, handleApprovalSuccess, clearEphemeralKey]
   202→  );
   203→
   204→  /**
   205→   * Create an approval request on the bulletin board (new device side).
   206→   * Generates an ephemeral keypair and starts polling.
   207→   *
   208→   * Uses a generation counter to handle React StrictMode double-mounting:
   209→   * the effect fires requestApproval twice in quick succession. Without the
   210→   * counter, both async calls race to set requestIdRef and start polling.
   211→   * If the first call's createRequest completes after the second's, it
   212→   * overwrites the ref and starts polling for a stale request (whose
   213→   * ephemeral key was already zeroed by cleanup), causing "Key unwrapping
   214→   * failed" when the approver encrypts the factor key with the stale
   215→   * request's public key.
   216→   */
   217→  const requestApproval = useCallback(async () => {
   218→    const gen = ++requestGenRef.current;
   219→
   220→    setApprovalStatus('requesting');
   221→    setApprovalError(null);
   222→
   223→    try {
   224→      // 1. Generate ephemeral secp256k1 keypair
   225→      // keygen() returns compressed pubkey (33 bytes) by default, but the
   226→      // backend DTO requires uncompressed (65 bytes = 130 hex chars).
   227→      const ephemeral = secp256k1.keygen();
   228→      ephemeralPrivKeyRef.current = ephemeral.secretKey;
   229→      const uncompressedPubKey = secp256k1.getPublicKey(ephemeral.secretKey, false);
   230→      const ephemeralPubKeyHex = bytesToHex(uncompressedPubKey);
   231→
   232→      // 2. Get device identity
   233→      const deviceIdentity = await getOrCreateDeviceIdentity();
   234→
   235→      // 3. Get device name
   236→      const deviceInfo = detectDeviceInfo();
   237→
   238→      // 4. Create request on bulletin board
   239→      const { requestId } = await deviceApprovalApi.createRequest({
   240→        deviceId: deviceIdentity.deviceId,
   241→        deviceName: deviceInfo.name,
   242→        ephemeralPublicKey: ephemeralPubKeyHex,
   243→      });
   244→
   245→      // 5. Check if this call was superseded by a newer requestApproval()
   246→      //    (happens in React StrictMode when the effect fires twice).
   247→      if (gen !== requestGenRef.current) {
   248→        // Stale: a newer requestApproval already started. Cancel this request
   249→        // on the backend and bail — the newer call will set its own refs.
   250→        try {
   251→          await deviceApprovalApi.cancel(requestId);
   252→        } catch {
   253→          /* best-effort */
   254→        }
   255→        return;
   256→      }
   257→
   258→      requestIdRef.current = requestId;
   259→      setApprovalStatus('pending');
   260→
   261→      // 6. Start polling for response
   262→      startRequesterPolling(requestId);
   263→    } catch (err) {
   264→      // Only handle errors for the active generation
   265→      if (gen !== requestGenRef.current) return;
   266→      clearEphemeralKey();
   267→      setApprovalError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to create approval request');
   268→      setApprovalStatus('error');
   269→    }
   270→  }, [startRequesterPolling, clearEphemeralKey]);
   271→
   272→  /**
   273→   * Cancel the pending approval request (new device side).
   274→   * Also called when recovery phrase is used instead (RESEARCH.md Pitfall 5).
   275→   *
   276→   * IMPORTANT: All synchronous state cleanup (stop polling, clear ephemeral key,
   277→   * null refs, reset status) MUST happen BEFORE the async API call. In React
   278→   * StrictMode, cleanup and setup run in quick succession — if requestApproval()
   279→   * generates a new ephemeral key between our `await cancel()` and
   280→   * `clearEphemeralKey()`, we'd zero the NEW key, causing "Key unwrapping failed"
   281→   * when the approve response arrives.
   282→   */
   283→  const cancelRequest = useCallback(async () => {
   284→    // Invalidate any in-flight requestApproval() completion immediately.
   285→    requestGenRef.current += 1;
   286→
   287→    stopRequesterPolling();
   288→    clearEphemeralKey();
   289→    setApprovalStatus('idle');
   290→
   291→    // Capture and clear requestId synchronously so a concurrent
   292→    // requestApproval() can safely set a new value on the ref.
   293→    const requestId = requestIdRef.current;
   294→    requestIdRef.current = null;
   295→
   296→    // Async API cleanup (best-effort, uses captured local variable)
   297→    if (requestId) {
   298→      try {
   299→        await deviceApprovalApi.cancel(requestId);
   300→      } catch {
   301→        // Best-effort cleanup -- request may already be expired/cancelled
   302→      }
   303→    }
   304→  }, [stopRequesterPolling, clearEphemeralKey]);
   305→
   306→  // =========================================================================
   307→  // EXISTING DEVICE SIDE (APPROVER)
   308→  // =========================================================================
   309→
   310→  /**
   311→   * Fetch pending requests from the bulletin board, filtering out recently
   312→   * handled ones to prevent stale in-flight responses from re-adding them.
   313→   */
   314→  const fetchFilteredPending = useCallback(async () => {
   315→    try {
   316→      const pending = await deviceApprovalApi.getPending();
   317→      // Guard against in-flight responses resolving after polling stopped
   318→      if (!isPollingPendingRef.current) return;
   319→      const handled = handledRequestIdsRef.current;
   320→      const filtered =
   321→        handled.size > 0
   322→          ? pending.filter((r) => r.requestId && !handled.has(r.requestId))
   323→          : pending;
   324→      setPendingRequests(filtered);
   325→    } catch {
   326→      // Network error -- continue polling
   327→    }
   328→  }, []);
   329→
   330→  /**
   331→   * Start polling for pending approval requests (existing device side).
   332→   */
   333→  const pollPendingRequests = useCallback(() => {
   334→    if (isPollingPendingRef.current) return;
   335→    isPollingPendingRef.current = true;
   336→
   337→    // Poll every 5 seconds
   338→    approverPollRef.current = setInterval(fetchFilteredPending, 5000);
   339→    // Fire immediately
   340→    void fetchFilteredPending();
   341→  }, [fetchFilteredPending]);
   342→
   343→  /**
   344→   * Approve a pending request: ECIES-encrypt current factor key with
   345→   * the requester's ephemeral public key and send the response.
   346→   */
   347→  const approveRequest = useCallback(
   348→    async (requestId: string, ephemeralPublicKeyHex: string) => {
   349→      if (!coreKit) throw new Error('Core Kit not initialized');
   350→
   351→      // 1. Get current factor key
   352→      const factorKeyResult = coreKit.getCurrentFactorKey();
   353→      if (!factorKeyResult?.factorKey) {
   354→        throw new Error('No active factor key available to share');
   355→      }
   356→      const factorKeyHex = factorKeyResult.factorKey.toString('hex').padStart(64, '0');
   357→      const factorKeyBytes = hexToBytes(factorKeyHex);
   358→
   359→      // 2. ECIES-encrypt with the requester's ephemeral public key
   360→      const ephemeralPubKey = hexToBytes(ephemeralPublicKeyHex);
   361→      const encrypted = await wrapKey(factorKeyBytes, ephemeralPubKey);
   362→      factorKeyBytes.fill(0); // Zero-fill factor key bytes after wrapping
   363→
   364→      // 3. Get current device ID for tracking
   365→      const deviceIdentity = await getOrCreateDeviceIdentity();
   366→
   367→      // 4. Send response
   368→      await deviceApprovalApi.respond(requestId, {
   369→        action: 'approve',
   370→        encryptedFactorKey: bytesToHex(encrypted),
   371→        respondedByDeviceId: deviceIdentity.deviceId,
   372→      });
   373→
   374→      // 5. Remove from pending list and mark as handled to prevent
   375→      //    stale polling responses from re-adding it
   376→      handledRequestIdsRef.current.add(requestId);
   377→      setPendingRequests((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.requestId !== requestId));
   378→    },
   379→    [coreKit]
   380→  );
   381→
   382→  /**
   383→   * Deny a pending approval request.
   384→   */
   385→  const denyRequest = useCallback(async (requestId: string) => {
   386→    // Get current device ID for tracking
   387→    const deviceIdentity = await getOrCreateDeviceIdentity();
   388→
   389→    await deviceApprovalApi.respond(requestId, {
   390→      action: 'deny',
   391→      respondedByDeviceId: deviceIdentity.deviceId,
   392→    });
   393→
   394→    // Remove from pending list and mark as handled
   395→    handledRequestIdsRef.current.add(requestId);
   396→    setPendingRequests((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.requestId !== requestId));
   397→  }, []);
   398→
   399→  // =========================================================================
   400→  // VISIBILITY-BASED POLLING CONTROL (approver)
   401→  // =========================================================================
   402→
   403→  // Pause/resume approver polling based on tab visibility
   404→  useEffect(() => {
   405→    if (!isPollingPendingRef.current) return;
   406→
   407→    if (isVisible) {
   408→      // Resume polling when tab becomes visible
   409→      if (!approverPollRef.current) {
   410→        approverPollRef.current = setInterval(fetchFilteredPending, 5000);
   411→        void fetchFilteredPending();
   412→      }
   413→    } else {
   414→      // Pause polling when tab is hidden
   415→      if (approverPollRef.current) {
   416→        clearInterval(approverPollRef.current);
   417→        approverPollRef.current = null;
   418→      }
   419→    }
   420→  }, [isVisible, fetchFilteredPending]);
   421→
   422→  // =========================================================================
   423→  // CLEANUP ON UNMOUNT
   424→  // =========================================================================
   425→
   426→  useEffect(() => {
   427→    return () => {
   428→      stopRequesterPolling();
   429→      stopApproverPolling();
   430→      clearEphemeralKey();
   431→    };
   432→  }, [stopRequesterPolling, stopApproverPolling, clearEphemeralKey]);
   433→
   434→  return {
   435→    // Requester (new device)
   436→    requestApproval,
   437→    cancelRequest,
   438→    approvalStatus,
   439→    approvalError,
   440→
   441→    // Approver (existing device)
   442→    pollPendingRequests,
   443→    stopApproverPolling,
   444→    approveRequest,
   445→    denyRequest,
   446→    pendingRequests,
   447→    isPolling: isPollingPendingRef.current,
   448→  };
   449→}
   450→
```

```
    18→export type FactorInfo = {

    20→  description: string;
    21→  type: string;

    25→
    26→export function useMfa() {
    27→  const { coreKit } = useCoreKit();
    28→
    29→  const isMfaEnabled = useMfaStore((s) => s.isMfaEnabled);
    30→  const isEnrolling = useMfaStore((s) => s.isEnrolling);
    31→  const factorCount = useMfaStore((s) => s.factorCount);
    32→  const threshold = useMfaStore((s) => s.threshold);
    33→
    34→  /**
    35→   * Check MFA status from Core Kit key details.
    36→   * MFA is enabled when totalFactors > 2 (every account starts with 2
    37→   * default factors: JWT verifier share + hashedShare cloud custodial key).
    38→   */
    39→  const checkMfaStatus = useCallback((): {
    40→    isMfaEnabled: boolean;
    41→    totalFactors: number;
    42→    threshold: number;
    43→  } => {
    44→    if (!coreKit || coreKit.status !== COREKIT_STATUS.LOGGED_IN) {
    45→      return { isMfaEnabled: false, totalFactors: 0, threshold: 0 };
    46→    }
    47→    try {
    48→      const details = coreKit.getKeyDetails();
    49→      const enabled = details.totalFactors > 2;
    50→      useMfaStore.getState().setMfaEnabled(enabled);
    51→      useMfaStore.getState().setFactorDetails(details.totalFactors, details.threshold);
    52→      return {
    53→        isMfaEnabled: enabled,
    54→        totalFactors: details.totalFactors,
    55→        threshold: details.threshold,
    56→      };
    57→    } catch {
    58→      return { isMfaEnabled: false, totalFactors: 0, threshold: 0 };
    59→    }
    60→  }, [coreKit]);
    61→
    62→  /**
    63→   * Enable MFA. Returns 24-word recovery mnemonic.
    64→   * CRITICAL: commitChanges() must be called before enableMFA() in manualSync mode.
    65→   */
    66→  const enableMfa = useCallback(async (): Promise<string> => {
    67→    if (!coreKit) throw new Error('Core Kit not initialized');
    68→    if (coreKit.status !== COREKIT_STATUS.LOGGED_IN) {
    69→      throw new Error('Must be logged in to enable MFA');
    70→    }
    71→
    72→    const store = useMfaStore.getState();
    73→    if (store.isMfaEnabled) {
    74→      throw new Error('MFA is already enabled');
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    useMfaStore.getState().setEnrolling(true);
    78→    try {
    79→      // Commit any pending changes first (CRITICAL for manualSync mode -- Pitfall 1)
    80→      await coreKit.commitChanges();
    81→
    82→      // Capture TSS public key BEFORE enableMFA for stability verification (MFA-04)
    83→      const preMfaTssPub = coreKit.getKeyDetails().tssPubKey;
    84→
    85→      // enableMFA creates device factor + recovery factor atomically
    86→      const backupFactorKeyHex = await coreKit.enableMFA({
    87→        shareDescription: FactorKeyTypeShareDescription.SeedPhrase,
    88→      });
    89→
    90→      // Commit the MFA changes
    91→      await coreKit.commitChanges();
    92→
    93→      // Defensive check: verify TSS public key unchanged after MFA enrollment (MFA-04)
    94→      const postMfaTssPub = coreKit.getKeyDetails().tssPubKey;
    95→      if (preMfaTssPub?.x && preMfaTssPub?.y && postMfaTssPub?.x && postMfaTssPub?.y) {
    96→        const preX = preMfaTssPub.x.toString('hex');
    97→        const preY = preMfaTssPub.y.toString('hex');
    98→        const postX = postMfaTssPub.x.toString('hex');
    99→        const postY = postMfaTssPub.y.toString('hex');
   100→        if (preX !== postX || preY !== postY) {
   101→          throw new Error(
   102→            'MFA enrollment failed: TSS public key changed. Your keypair identity is no longer stable. Please contact support.'
   103→          );
   104→        }
   105→      }
   106→
   107→      // Convert to 24-word mnemonic for user display
   108→      const mnemonic = keyToMnemonic(backupFactorKeyHex);
   109→
   110→      // Update store with new key details
   111→      const details = coreKit.getKeyDetails();
   112→      useMfaStore.getState().setMfaEnabled(true);
   113→      useMfaStore.getState().setFactorDetails(details.totalFactors, details.threshold);
   114→
   115→      return mnemonic;
   116→    } finally {
   117→      useMfaStore.getState().setEnrolling(false);
   118→    }
   119→  }, [coreKit]);
   120→
   121→  /**
   122→   * Input a factor key (hex string) to complete REQUIRED_SHARE login.
   123→   * Used for both recovery and cross-device approval flows.
   124→   *
   125→   * IMPORTANT: Does NOT call syncStatus() because doing so would transition
   126→   * Core Kit's React context to LOGGED_IN, which triggers the session
   127→   * restoration effect in useAuth.ts. Since backend auth hasn't completed yet
   128→   * (no valid access token or refresh cookie), the session restore fails and
   129→   * calls coreKitLogout(), effectively undoing the recovery.
   130→   *
   131→   * The caller (completeRequiredShare) is responsible for syncing status
   132→   * AFTER backend auth completes.
   133→   */
   134→  const inputFactorKey = useCallback(
   135→    async (factorKeyHex: string): Promise<void> => {
   136→      if (!coreKit) throw new Error('Core Kit not initialized');
   137→      const factorKey = new BN(factorKeyHex, 'hex');
   138→      await coreKit.inputFactorKey(factorKey);
   139→      // syncStatus() deliberately omitted — see comment above
   140→    },
   141→    [coreKit]
   142→  );
   143→
   144→  /**
   145→   * Recover with a 24-word mnemonic phrase.
   146→   * After recovery, creates a device factor for the new device.
   147→   */
   148→  const recoverWithMnemonic = useCallback(
   149→    async (mnemonic: string): Promise<void> => {
   150→      if (!coreKit) throw new Error('Core Kit not initialized');
   151→
   152→      // Convert mnemonic to factor key hex
   153→      const factorKeyHex = mnemonicToKey(mnemonic.trim().toLowerCase());
   154→      await inputFactorKey(factorKeyHex);
   155→
   156→      // Create a device factor for this new device with metadata
   157→      const newDeviceFactor = generateFactorKey();
   158→      const deviceKeypair = await getOrCreateDeviceIdentity();
   159→      const deviceInfo = detectDeviceInfo();
   160→      await coreKit.createFactor({
   161→        shareType: TssShareType.DEVICE,
   162→        factorKey: newDeviceFactor.private,
   163→        shareDescription: FactorKeyTypeShareDescription.DeviceShare,
   164→        additionalMetadata: {
   165→          deviceId: deviceKeypair.deviceId,
   166→          browserName: deviceInfo.name,
   167→        },
   168→      });
   169→      await coreKit.setDeviceFactor(newDeviceFactor.private);
   170→      await coreKit.commitChanges();
   171→    },
   172→    [coreKit, inputFactorKey]
   173→  );
   174→
   175→  /**
   176→   * Get all factors with their descriptions.
   177→   * shareDescriptions is Record<string, string[]> where keys are factor pub hex.
   178→   */
   179→  const getFactors = useCallback((): FactorInfo[] => {
   180→    if (!coreKit || coreKit.status !== COREKIT_STATUS.LOGGED_IN) return [];
   181→    try {
   182→      const details = coreKit.getKeyDetails();
   183→      const factors: FactorInfo[] = [];
   184→
   185→      // Known system fields stored by Web3Auth in the flat JSON description.
   186→      // Everything else is user-provided additionalMetadata (spread at top level by addFactorDescription).
   187→      const systemFields = new Set(['module', 'dateAdded', 'tssShareIndex', 'tssIndex']);
   188→
   189→      for (const [factorPubHex, descriptions] of Object.entries(details.shareDescriptions)) {
   190→        for (const descJson of descriptions) {
   191→          try {
   192→            const parsed = JSON.parse(descJson) as Record<string, unknown>;
   193→            const module = (parsed.module as string) || 'unknown';
   194→            const tssShareIndex = parsed.tssShareIndex as number | undefined;
   195→
   196→            // Normalize type: enableMFA() creates factors with module="Other"
   197→            // but tssShareIndex correctly identifies 2=DEVICE, 3=RECOVERY
   198→            let type = module;
   199→            if (module === FactorKeyTypeShareDescription.Other || module === 'Other') {
   200→              if (tssShareIndex === 2) {
   201→                type = FactorKeyTypeShareDescription.DeviceShare;
   202→              } else if (tssShareIndex === 3) {
   203→                type = FactorKeyTypeShareDescription.SeedPhrase;
   204→              }
   205→            }
   206→
   207→            // Extract additionalMetadata from flat JSON (Web3Auth spreads it at top level)
   208→            const additionalMetadata: Record<string, string> = {};
   209→            for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(parsed)) {
   210→              if (!systemFields.has(key) && typeof value === 'string') {
   211→                additionalMetadata[key] = value;
   212→              }
   213→            }
   214→
   215→            factors.push({
   216→              factorPubHex,
   217→              description: type,
   218→              type,
   219→              additionalMetadata:
   220→                Object.keys(additionalMetadata).length > 0 ? additionalMetadata : undefined,
   221→            });
   222→          } catch {
   223→            factors.push({
   224→              factorPubHex,
   225→              description: 'unknown',
   226→              type: 'unknown',
   227→            });
   228→          }
   229→        }
   230→      }
   231→
   232→      return factors;
   233→    } catch {
   234→      return [];
   235→    }
   236→  }, [coreKit]);
   237→
   238→  /**
   239→   * Delete a factor by its public key hex.
   240→   * Cannot delete the currently active factor or the last remaining factor.
   241→   */
   242→  const deleteFactor = useCallback(
   243→    async (factorPubHex: string): Promise<void> => {
   244→      if (!coreKit) throw new Error('Core Kit not initialized');
   245→      const factorPub = Point.fromSEC1(factorKeyCurve, factorPubHex);
   246→      await coreKit.deleteFactor(factorPub);
   247→      await coreKit.commitChanges();
   248→    },
   249→    [coreKit]
   250→  );
   251→
   252→  /**
   253→   * Get the current active factor key and share type.
   254→   */
   255→  const getCurrentFactorKey = useCallback((): {
   256→    factorKey: BN;
   257→    shareType: TssShareType;
   258→  } | null => {
   259→    if (!coreKit || coreKit.status !== COREKIT_STATUS.LOGGED_IN) return null;
   260→    try {
   261→      return coreKit.getCurrentFactorKey();
   262→    } catch {
   263→      return null;
   264→    }
   265→  }, [coreKit]);
   266→
   267→  /**
   268→   * Regenerate the recovery phrase.
   269→   * Deletes the old recovery factor and creates a new one.
   270→   * Returns the new 24-word mnemonic.
   271→   */
   272→  const regenerateRecoveryPhrase = useCallback(async (): Promise<string> => {
   273→    if (!coreKit) throw new Error('Core Kit not initialized');
   274→    if (coreKit.status !== COREKIT_STATUS.LOGGED_IN) {
   275→      throw new Error('Must be logged in to regenerate recovery phrase');
   276→    }
   277→
   278→    // Find the existing recovery (seedPhrase) factor
   279→    const factors = getFactors();
   280→    const recoveryFactor = factors.find((f) => f.type === FactorKeyTypeShareDescription.SeedPhrase);
   281→
   282→    if (recoveryFactor) {
   283→      // Delete old recovery factor
   284→      const factorPub = Point.fromSEC1(factorKeyCurve, recoveryFactor.factorPubHex);
   285→      await coreKit.deleteFactor(factorPub);
   286→    }
   287→
   288→    // Generate new recovery factor
   289→    const newRecovery = generateFactorKey();
   290→    await coreKit.createFactor({
   291→      shareType: TssShareType.RECOVERY,
   292→      factorKey: newRecovery.private,
   293→      shareDescription: FactorKeyTypeShareDescription.SeedPhrase,
   294→    });
   295→    await coreKit.commitChanges();
   296→
   297→    // Convert the new factor key to mnemonic
   298→    // Zero-pad to 64 hex chars (32 bytes)
   299→    const factorKeyHex = newRecovery.private.toString('hex').padStart(64, '0');
   300→    return keyToMnemonic(factorKeyHex);
   301→  }, [coreKit, getFactors]);
   302→
   303→  return {
   304→    // State from store
   305→    isMfaEnabled,
   306→    isEnrolling,
   307→    factorCount,
   308→    threshold,
   309→
   310→    // Operations
   311→    checkMfaStatus,
   312→    enableMfa,

   314→    recoverWithMnemonic,
   315→    getFactors,
   316→    deleteFactor,
   317→    getCurrentFactorKey,
   318→    regenerateRecoveryPhrase,
   319→  };
   320→}
```

```
     1→import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';
     3→import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
     4→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
     5→import { useQuotaStore } from '../stores/quota.store';
     6→import { unpinFromIpfs } from '../lib/api/ipfs';
     7→import * as folderService from '../services/folder.service';

     9→import {

    11→  resolveFileMetadata,
    12→  updateFileMetadata,
```
