```
    15→} from '../services/file-metadata.service';
    16→import type { FileIpnsRecordPayload } from '../services/file-metadata.service';
    17→import type { FolderChild, FilePointer } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    18→import { unwrapKey, hexToBytes } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    19→import { getRootFolderState, resyncFolder, withConflictRetry } from './folder-helpers';
    20→import type { FolderOperationState } from './folder-helpers';
    21→
    22→/**
    23→ * React hook for file add/update operations.
    24→ *
    25→ * Returns loading/error state and operation callbacks.
    26→ */
    27→export function useFileOperations() {
    28→  const [state, setState] = useState<FolderOperationState>({
    29→    isLoading: false,
    30→    error: null,
    31→  });
    32→
    33→  /**
    34→   * Add a file to a folder after upload (v2).
    35→   *
    36→   * Creates per-file IPNS metadata, then registers FilePointer in folder.
    37→   *
    38→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID ('root' or folder UUID)
    39→   * @param fileData - Uploaded file data from upload service
    40→   * @returns Created file pointer
    41→   */
    42→  const handleAddFile = useCallback(
    43→    async (
    44→      parentId: string,
    45→      fileData: {
    46→        cid: string;
    47→        wrappedKey: string;
    48→        iv: string;
    49→        originalName: string;
    50→        originalSize: number;
    51→        mimeType?: string;
    52→        encryptionMode?: 'GCM' | 'CTR';
    53→      }
    54→    ): Promise<FilePointer> => {
    55→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
    56→      try {
    57→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
    58→        const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
    59→        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
    60→
    61→        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
    62→          throw new Error('No ECIES keypair available - please log in again');
    63→        }
    64→
    65→        // Get parent folder state
    66→        const parentFolder =
    67→          parentId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[parentId];
    68→
    69→        if (!parentFolder) {
    70→          throw new Error('Parent folder not found or vault not initialized');
    71→        }
    72→
    73→        // 1. Generate fileId and create per-file IPNS metadata
    74→        const fileId = crypto.randomUUID();
    75→        const { ipnsRecord, ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } = await createFileMetadata({
    76→          fileId,
    77→          cid: fileData.cid,
    78→          fileKeyEncrypted: fileData.wrappedKey,
    79→          fileIv: fileData.iv,
    80→          size: fileData.originalSize,
    81→          mimeType: fileData.mimeType ?? 'application/octet-stream',
    82→          folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
    83→          userPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
    84→          encryptionMode: fileData.encryptionMode,
    85→        });
    86→
    87→        // 2. Register FilePointer in folder (batch publishes file + folder IPNS)
    88→        //    On 409 conflict, re-sync the parent folder and retry once.
    89→        const performAddFile = async (): Promise<{
    90→          filePointer: FilePointer;
    91→          newSequenceNumber: bigint;
    92→        }> => {
    93→          const freshFolders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
    94→          const freshVault = useVaultStore.getState();
    95→          const freshParent =
    96→            parentId === 'root'
    97→              ? getRootFolderState(freshVault, freshFolders)
    98→              : freshFolders[parentId];
    99→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   100→
   101→          return folderService.addFileToFolder({
   102→            parentFolderState: freshParent,
   103→            fileId,
   104→            name: fileData.originalName,
   105→            fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
   106→            ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   107→          });
   108→        };
   109→
   110→        const { filePointer, newSequenceNumber } = await withConflictRetry(performAddFile, () =>
   111→          resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id)
   112→        );
   113→
   114→        // 3. Update local state with new child and sequence number
   115→        const freshFolders2 = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   116→        const freshVault2 = useVaultStore.getState();
   117→        const freshParent2 =
   118→          parentId === 'root'
   119→            ? getRootFolderState(freshVault2, freshFolders2)
   120→            : freshFolders2[parentId];
   121→        const updatedChildren: FolderChild[] = [...(freshParent2?.children ?? []), filePointer];
   122→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   123→        store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   124→        store.updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
   125→
   126→        // 4. Post-upload: re-wrap file key for share recipients (fire-and-forget)
   127→        // Unwrap the fileKey from the owner-wrapped key, then delegate to reWrapForRecipients
   128→        (async () => {
   129→          try {
   130→            const authState = useAuthStore.getState();
   131→            if (!authState.vaultKeypair) return;
   132→            const fileKey = await unwrapKey(
   133→              hexToBytes(fileData.wrappedKey),
   134→              authState.vaultKeypair.privateKey
   135→            );
   136→            try {
   137→              await reWrapForRecipients({
   138→                folderIpnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   139→                folders: useFolderStore.getState().folders,
   140→                currentFolderId: parentFolder.id,
   141→                newItems: [{ keyType: 'file', itemId: fileId, plaintextKey: fileKey }],
   142→              });
   143→            } finally {
   144→              fileKey.fill(0);
   145→            }
   146→          } catch (err) {
   147→            console.warn('[share] Post-upload file re-wrapping failed:', err);
   148→          }
   149→        })();
   150→
   151→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   152→        return filePointer;
   153→      } catch (err) {
   154→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to add file';
   155→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   156→        throw err;
   157→      }
   158→    },
   159→    []
   160→  );
   161→
   162→  /**
   163→   * Add multiple files to a folder after upload (v2 batch).

   172→  const handleAddFiles = useCallback(

   185→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });

   187→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;

   191→        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
   192→          throw new Error('No ECIES keypair available - please log in again');
   193→        }
   194→
   195→        // Get parent folder state

   198→
   199→        if (!parentFolder) {

   208→            const { ipnsRecord, ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } = await createFileMetadata({
   209→              fileId,
   210→              cid: f.cid,
   211→              fileKeyEncrypted: f.wrappedKey,
   212→              fileIv: f.iv,
   213→              size: f.originalSize,
   214→              mimeType: f.mimeType ?? 'application/octet-stream',
   215→              folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
   216→              userPublicKey,
   217→              encryptionMode: f.encryptionMode,
   218→            });
   219→            return {
   220→              fileId,
   221→              name: f.originalName,
   222→              fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
   223→              ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
   224→            };
   225→          })
   226→        );
   227→
   228→        // 2. Register FilePointers in folder (batch publishes all IPNS records)
   229→        //    On 409 conflict, re-sync the parent folder and retry once.
   230→        const performAddFiles = async (): Promise<{
   231→          filePointers: FilePointer[];
   232→          newSequenceNumber: bigint;
   233→        }> => {
   234→          const freshFolders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   235→          const freshVault = useVaultStore.getState();
   236→          const freshParent =
   237→            parentId === 'root'
   238→              ? getRootFolderState(freshVault, freshFolders)
   239→              : freshFolders[parentId];
   240→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   241→
   242→          return folderService.addFilesToFolder({
   243→            parentFolderState: freshParent,
   244→            files: filesWithRecords,
   245→          });
   246→        };
   247→
   248→        const { filePointers, newSequenceNumber } = await withConflictRetry(performAddFiles, () =>
   249→          resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id)
   250→        );
   251→
   252→        // 3. Update local state with new children and sequence number
   253→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   254→        const freshFolders2 = store.folders;
   255→        const freshVault2 = useVaultStore.getState();
   256→        const freshParent2 =
   257→          parentId === 'root'
   258→            ? getRootFolderState(freshVault2, freshFolders2)
   259→            : freshFolders2[parentId];
   260→        store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, [...(freshParent2?.children ?? []), ...filePointers]);
   261→        store.updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
   262→
   263→        // 4. Post-upload: re-wrap file keys for share recipients (fire-and-forget)
   264→        (async () => {
   265→          const newItems: Array<{
   266→            keyType: 'file' | 'folder';
   267→            itemId: string;
   268→            plaintextKey: Uint8Array;
   269→          }> = [];
   270→          try {
   271→            const authState = useAuthStore.getState();
   272→            if (!authState.vaultKeypair) return;
   273→            const privateKey = authState.vaultKeypair.privateKey;
   274→            for (let i = 0; i < filesData.length; i++) {
   275→              const fileKey = await unwrapKey(hexToBytes(filesData[i].wrappedKey), privateKey);
   276→              newItems.push({
   277→                keyType: 'file',
   278→                itemId: filesWithRecords[i].fileId,
   279→                plaintextKey: fileKey,
   280→              });
   281→            }
   282→            await reWrapForRecipients({
   283→              folderIpnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   284→              folders: useFolderStore.getState().folders,
   285→              currentFolderId: parentFolder.id,
   286→              newItems,
   287→            });
   288→          } catch (err) {
   289→            console.warn('[share] Post-upload batch file re-wrapping failed:', err);
   290→          } finally {
   291→            for (const item of newItems) {
   292→              item.plaintextKey.fill(0);
   293→            }
   294→          }
   295→        })();
   296→
   297→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   298→        return filePointers;
   299→      } catch (err) {
   300→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to add files';
   301→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   302→        throw err;
   303→      }
   304→    },
   305→    []
   306→  );
   307→
   308→  /**
   309→   * Update a file's content in-place (v2: re-encrypt, update file IPNS, folder untouched).
   310→   *
   311→   * Old CIDs are preserved as version history (VER-01). Only pruned versions
   312→   * (excess beyond max 10) have their CIDs unpinned.
   313→   *
   314→   * NOTE: No conflict detection here -- handleUpdateFile publishes only the
   315→   * per-file IPNS record. Folder metadata is NOT touched, so no 409 is possible.
   316→   *
   317→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID ('root' or folder UUID)
   318→   * @param fileData - New file data after re-encryption
   319→   * @returns Resolves when the update is complete
   320→   */
   321→  const handleUpdateFile = useCallback(
   322→    async (
   323→      parentId: string,
   324→      fileData: {
   325→        fileId: string;
   326→        newCid: string;
   327→        newFileKeyEncrypted: string;
   328→        newFileIv: string;
   329→        newSize: number;
   330→        forceVersion?: boolean; // true for web re-upload, false/undefined for text editor
   331→      }
   332→    ): Promise<void> => {
   333→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   334→      try {
   335→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   336→        const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
   337→        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   338→
   339→        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
   340→          throw new Error('No ECIES keypair available - please log in again');
   341→        }
   342→
   343→        // Get parent folder state
   344→        const parentFolder =
   345→          parentId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[parentId];
   346→
   347→        if (!parentFolder) {
   348→          throw new Error('Parent folder not found or vault not initialized');
   349→        }
   350→
   351→        // 1. Find the FilePointer in parent's children
   352→        const filePointer = parentFolder.children.find(
   353→          (c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === fileData.fileId
   354→        ) as FilePointer | undefined;
   355→
   356→        if (!filePointer) {
   357→          throw new Error('File not found in folder');
   358→        }
   359→
   360→        // 2. Resolve current file metadata from IPNS
   361→        const { metadata: currentMetadata } = await resolveFileMetadata(
   362→          filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
   363→          parentFolder.folderKey
   364→        );
   365→
   366→        // 3. Decrypt the file IPNS private key from FilePointer (or HKDF fallback)
   367→        const { privateKey: fileIpnsPrivateKey, migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } =
   368→          await getFileIpnsPrivateKey(
   369→            filePointer,
   370→            auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   371→            auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey
   372→          );
   373→
   374→        // 4. Determine whether to create a version entry
   375→        const createVersion = shouldCreateVersion(currentMetadata, fileData.forceVersion ?? false);
   376→
   377→        let ipnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
   378→        let prunedCids: string[];
   379→        try {
   380→          // 5. Update file metadata and publish new IPNS record
   381→          ({ ipnsRecord, prunedCids } = await updateFileMetadata({
   382→            fileIpnsPrivateKey,
   383→            fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
   384→            folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
   385→            currentMetadata,
   386→            updates: {
   387→              cid: fileData.newCid,
   388→              fileKeyEncrypted: fileData.newFileKeyEncrypted,
   389→              fileIv: fileData.newFileIv,
   390→              size: fileData.newSize,
   391→            },
   392→            createVersion,
   393→          }));
   394→        } finally {
   395→          fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
   396→        }
   397→
   398→        // 6. Publish only the file IPNS record (folder metadata untouched!)
   399→        await folderService.replaceFileInFolder({
   400→          fileId: fileData.fileId,
   401→          fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
   402→          parentFolderState: parentFolder,
   403→        });
   404→
   405→        // 7. Update local state -- touch modifiedAt and persist migrated IPNS key if applicable
   406→        const updatedChildren = parentFolder.children.map((child) => {
   407→          if (child.type === 'file' && child.id === fileData.fileId) {
   408→            return {
   409→              ...child,
   410→              modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   411→              ...(migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted
   412→                ? { ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted }
   413→                : {}),
   414→            };
   415→          }
   416→          return child;
   417→        });
   418→
   419→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   420→        store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   421→
   422→        // 7b. Lazy migration: persist wrapped IPNS key to folder metadata on IPFS
   423→        if (migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted) {
   424→          folderService
   425→            .updateFolderMetadata({
   426→              folderId: parentFolder.id,
   427→              children: updatedChildren,
   428→              folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
   429→              ipnsPrivateKey: parentFolder.ipnsPrivateKey,
   430→              ipnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   431→              sequenceNumber: parentFolder.sequenceNumber,
   432→            })
   433→            .then(({ newSequenceNumber }) => {
   434→              useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
   435→            })
   436→            .catch((err) => {
   437→              console.warn('Lazy IPNS key migration: folder re-publish failed, will retry:', err);
   438→            });
   439→        }
   440→
   441→        // 8. Only unpin CIDs of pruned versions (excess beyond max 10)
   442→        // Old CIDs stay pinned as version history (VER-01)
   443→        for (const prunedCid of prunedCids) {
   444→          unpinFromIpfs(prunedCid).catch(() => {});
   445→        }
   446→
   447→        // Refresh quota
   448→        useQuotaStore.getState().fetchQuota();
   449→
   450→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   451→      } catch (err) {
   452→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to update file';
   453→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   454→        throw err;
   455→      }
   456→    },
   457→    []
   458→  );
   459→
   460→  return {
   461→    ...state,
   462→    addFile: handleAddFile,
   463→    addFiles: handleAddFiles,
   464→    updateFile: handleUpdateFile,
   465→  };
   466→}
   467→
```

```
    18→/**
    19→ * Build a breadcrumb-style path string for a folder by walking up the tree.
    20→ * e.g., "My Vault / Documents / Reports"
    21→ */
    22→function buildFolderPath(folderId: string): string {
    23→  const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
    24→  const parts: string[] = [];
    25→  let currentId: string | null = folderId;
    26→
    27→  while (currentId !== null) {
    28→    const folder: FolderNode | undefined = folders[currentId];
    29→    if (!folder) break;
    30→    // Stop before including the root node (its name is already the "My Vault" prefix)
    31→    if (folder.parentId === null) break;
    32→    parts.unshift(folder.name);
    33→    currentId = folder.parentId;
    34→  }
    35→
    36→  return parts.length > 0 ? `My Vault / ${parts.join(' / ')}` : 'My Vault';
    37→}

   328→        if (itemType === 'folder') {

   330→          const freshFolders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   331→          const movedFolder = freshFolders[itemId];
   332→          if (movedFolder) {
   333→            useFolderStore.getState().setFolder({
   334→              ...movedFolder,
   335→              parentId: destParentId,
   336→            });
   337→          }
   338→        }
   339→
   340→        // Update source folder's children (remove item)
   341→        if (freshSourceBeforeUpdate) {
   342→          const updatedSourceChildren = freshSourceBeforeUpdate.children.filter(
   343→            (c) => c.id !== itemId
   344→          );
   345→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(sourceParentId, updatedSourceChildren);
   346→        }
   347→
   348→        // Update dest folder's children (add item)
   349→        const freshDest = getDestFolder();
   350→        if (movedItem && freshDest) {
   351→          const updatedDestChildren = [
   352→            ...freshDest.children,
   353→            { ...movedItem, modifiedAt: Date.now() },
   354→          ];
   355→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(destParentId, updatedDestChildren);
   356→        }
   357→
   358→        // Persist the new sequence numbers so subsequent operations use the correct values
   359→        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(destParentId, moveResult.destSequenceNumber);
   360→        useFolderStore
   361→          .getState()
   362→          .updateFolderSequence(sourceParentId, moveResult.sourceSequenceNumber);
   363→
   364→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   365→      } catch (err) {
   366→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to move';
   367→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   368→        throw err;
   369→      }
   370→    },
   371→    []
   372→  );
   373→
   374→  /**
   375→   * Move multiple files/folders to a destination in a single batch.
   376→   *
   377→   * Uses add-before-remove pattern. Publishes IPNS once for the destination
   378→   * and once for the source (2 total), regardless of how many items are moved.
   379→   *
   380→   * @param items - Array of { id, type } to move
   381→   * @param sourceParentId - Current parent folder ID (all items must share the same parent)
   382→   * @param destParentId - Destination parent folder ID
   383→   */
   384→  const handleMoveItems = useCallback(
   385→    async (
   386→      items: Array<{ id: string; type: 'file' | 'folder' }>,
   387→      sourceParentId: string,
   388→      destParentId: string
   389→    ): Promise<void> => {
   390→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   391→      try {
   392→        const getSourceFolder = () => {
   393→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   394→          return sourceParentId === 'root'
   395→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   396→            : freshState.folders[sourceParentId];
   397→        };
   398→        const getDestFolder = () => {
   399→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   400→          return destParentId === 'root'
   401→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   402→            : freshState.folders[destParentId];
   403→        };
   404→
   405→        const sourceFolder = getSourceFolder();
   406→        const destFolder = getDestFolder();
   407→
   408→        if (!sourceFolder || !destFolder) {
   409→          throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   410→        }
   411→
   412→        const itemIds = new Set(items.map((i) => i.id));
   413→        const now = Date.now();
   414→
   415→        // Validate batch move preconditions against current folder state.
   416→        // Must be called before each attempt (initial + retry after resync)
   417→        // because concurrent changes may introduce name collisions or depth violations.
   418→        const validateBatchMove = () => {
   419→          const currentSource = getSourceFolder();
   420→          const currentDest = getDestFolder();
   421→          if (!currentSource || !currentDest) {
   422→            throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   423→          }
   424→
   425→          const currentMovedChildren = currentSource.children.filter((c) => itemIds.has(c.id));
   426→          if (currentMovedChildren.length !== itemIds.size) {
   427→            throw new Error(
   428→              'One or more selected items no longer exist. Please refresh and retry.'
   429→            );
   430→          }
   431→          const batchNames = new Set<string>();
   432→          for (const child of currentMovedChildren) {
   433→            // Intra-batch duplicate name check
   434→            if (batchNames.has(child.name)) {
   435→              throw new Error(`Multiple selected items share the name "${child.name}"`);
   436→            }
   437→            batchNames.add(child.name);
   438→
   439→            // Name collision check against destination
   440→            const nameExists = currentDest.children.some((c) => c.name === child.name);
   441→            if (nameExists) {
   442→              throw new Error(`An item named "${child.name}" already exists in the destination`);
   443→            }
   444→
   445→            if (child.type === 'folder') {
   446→              // Prevent moving folder into itself or descendant
   447→              const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   448→              if (folderService.isDescendantOf(currentDest.id, child.id, folders)) {
   449→                throw new Error(`Cannot move "${child.name}" into itself or its subfolder`);
   450→              }
   451→
   452→              // Depth limit check
   453→              const destDepth = folderService.getDepth(currentDest.id, folders);
   454→              const subtreeDepth = folderService.calculateSubtreeDepth(child.id, folders);
   455→              if (destDepth + 1 + subtreeDepth > MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH) {
   456→                throw new Error(
   457→                  `Cannot move "${child.name}": would exceed maximum folder depth of ${MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH}`
   458→                );
   459→              }
   460→            }
   461→          }
   462→        };
   463→
   464→        validateBatchMove();
   465→
   466→        const performBatchMove = async (): Promise<{
   467→          destSeq: bigint;
   468→          sourceSeq: bigint;
   469→          destChildren: FolderNode['children'];
   470→          sourceChildren: FolderNode['children'];
   471→        }> => {
   472→          const freshSource = getSourceFolder();
   473→          const freshDest = getDestFolder();
   474→          if (!freshSource || !freshDest) throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   475→
   476→          // Rebuild moved children from fresh source state
   477→          const freshMoved = freshSource.children.filter((c) => itemIds.has(c.id));
   478→          if (freshMoved.length !== itemIds.size) {
   479→            throw new Error(
   480→              'One or more selected items no longer exist. Please refresh and retry.'
   481→            );
   482→          }
   483→
   484→          // ADD all to destination FIRST (add-before-remove pattern)
   485→          const destChildren = [
   486→            ...freshDest.children,
   487→            ...freshMoved.map((c) => ({ ...c, modifiedAt: now })),
   488→          ];
   489→
   490→          const { newSequenceNumber: destSeq } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   491→            folderId: freshDest.id,
   492→            children: destChildren,
   493→            folderKey: freshDest.folderKey,
   494→            ipnsPrivateKey: freshDest.ipnsPrivateKey,
   495→            ipnsName: freshDest.ipnsName,
   496→            sequenceNumber: freshDest.sequenceNumber,
   497→          });
   498→
   499→          // REMOVE all from source AFTER destination confirmed
   500→          const sourceChildren = freshSource.children.filter((c) => !itemIds.has(c.id));
   501→
   502→          const { newSequenceNumber: sourceSeq } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   503→            folderId: freshSource.id,
   504→            children: sourceChildren,
   505→            folderKey: freshSource.folderKey,
   506→            ipnsPrivateKey: freshSource.ipnsPrivateKey,
   507→            ipnsName: freshSource.ipnsName,
   508→            // Re-read sequence in case source === dest parent was updated above
   509→            sequenceNumber: freshSource.id === freshDest.id ? destSeq : freshSource.sequenceNumber,
   510→          });
   511→
   512→          return { destSeq, sourceSeq, destChildren, sourceChildren };
   513→        };
   514→
   515→        const result = await withConflictRetry(
   516→          performBatchMove,
   517→          async () => {
   518→            await Promise.all([
   519→              resyncFolder(sourceFolder.ipnsName, sourceFolder.id),
   520→              sourceFolder.id !== destFolder.id
   521→                ? resyncFolder(destFolder.ipnsName, destFolder.id)
   522→                : Promise.resolve(),
   523→            ]);
   524→          },
   525→          validateBatchMove
   526→        );
   527→
   528→        // Update local state
   529→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   530→        store.updateFolderChildren(sourceParentId, result.sourceChildren);
   531→        store.updateFolderChildren(destParentId, result.destChildren);
   532→        store.updateFolderSequence(destParentId, result.destSeq);
   533→        store.updateFolderSequence(sourceParentId, result.sourceSeq);
   534→
   535→        for (const item of items) {
   536→          if (item.type === 'folder') {
   537→            const movedFolder = useFolderStore.getState().folders[item.id];
   538→            if (movedFolder) {
   539→              store.setFolder({ ...movedFolder, parentId: destParentId });
   540→            }
   541→          }
   542→        }
   543→
   544→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   545→      } catch (err) {
   546→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to move items';
   547→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   548→        throw err;
   549→      }
   550→    },
   551→    []
   552→  );
   553→
   554→  /**
   555→   * Delete a file or folder.
   556→   *
   557→   * @param itemId - ID of item to delete
   558→   * @param itemType - 'file' or 'folder'
   559→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID
   560→   */
   561→  const handleDelete = useCallback(
   562→    async (itemId: string, itemType: 'file' | 'folder', parentId: string): Promise<void> => {
   563→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   564→      try {
   565→        const getParentFolder = () => {
   566→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   567→          return parentId === 'root'
   568→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   569→            : freshState.folders[parentId];
   570→        };
   571→
   572→        const parentFolder = getParentFolder();
   573→        if (!parentFolder) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   574→
   575→        const performDelete = async (): Promise<{
   576→          newSequenceNumber: bigint;
   577→          removedChild: FolderChild;
   578→        }> => {
   579→          const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   580→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   581→          if (itemType === 'folder') {
   582→            const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   583→            const { newSequenceNumber, removedChild } = await folderService.deleteFolder({
   584→              folderId: itemId,
   585→              parentFolderState: freshParent,
   586→              getFolderState: (id) => folders[id],
   587→            });
   588→            return { newSequenceNumber, removedChild };
   589→          } else {
   590→            const { newSequenceNumber, removedChild } = await folderService.deleteFileFromFolder({
   591→              fileId: itemId,
   592→              parentFolderState: freshParent,
   593→            });
   594→            return { newSequenceNumber, removedChild };
   595→          }
   596→        };
   597→
   598→        const deleteResult = await withConflictRetry(performDelete, () =>
   599→          resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id)
   600→        );
   601→
   602→        if (itemType === 'folder') {
   603→          // Remove folder and all loaded descendants from local state
   604→          const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   605→          const removeRecursive = (folderId: string) => {
   606→            const node = store.folders[folderId];
   607→            if (node) {
   608→              for (const child of node.children) {
   609→                if (child.type === 'folder') removeRecursive(child.id);
   610→              }
   611→            }
   612→            store.removeFolder(folderId);
   613→          };
   614→          removeRecursive(itemId);
   615→        }
   616→
   617→        // Update parent's children (remove item) using fresh state
   618→        const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   619→        if (freshParent) {
   620→          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.filter((c) => c.id !== itemId);
   621→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   622→        }
   623→
   624→        // Persist the new sequence number so subsequent operations use the correct value
   625→        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(parentId, deleteResult.newSequenceNumber);
   626→
   627→        // Fire-and-forget: add deleted item to recycle bin (CIDs stay pinned for recovery)
   628→        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   629→        if (auth.vaultKeypair) {
   630→          void addManyToBin({
   631→            items: [deleteResult.removedChild],
   632→            parentIpnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   633→            parentPath: buildFolderPath(parentId),
   634→            userPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
   635→            userPrivateKey: auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   636→          }).catch(() => {
   637→            console.error('[Delete] Failed to add to bin (non-blocking)');
   638→          });
   639→        }
   640→
   641→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   642→      } catch (err) {
   643→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete';
   644→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   645→        throw err;
   646→      }
   647→    },
   648→    []
   649→  );
   650→
   651→  /**
   652→   * Delete multiple files/folders in a single IPNS publish.
   653→   *
   654→   * Removes all items from parent's children array, collects CIDs to unpin,
   655→   * then publishes metadata and IPNS once for the entire batch.
   656→   *
   657→   * @param items - Array of { id, type } to delete
   658→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID (all items must share the same parent)
   659→   */
   660→  const handleDeleteItems = useCallback(
   661→    async (
   662→      items: Array<{ id: string; type: 'file' | 'folder' }>,
   663→      parentId: string
   664→    ): Promise<void> => {
   665→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   666→      try {
   667→        const getParentFolder = () => {
   668→          const freshState = useFolderStore.getState();
   669→          return parentId === 'root'
   670→            ? getRootFolderState(useVaultStore.getState(), freshState.folders)
   671→            : freshState.folders[parentId];
   672→        };
   673→
   674→        const parentFolder = getParentFolder();
   675→        if (!parentFolder) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   676→
   677→        const itemIds = new Set(items.map((i) => i.id));
   678→
   679→        // Collect nested folder IDs to remove from store
   680→        // In v2, file children are FilePointers (no inline CID). File IPNS/TEE
   681→        // enrollments will expire naturally (24h IPNS lifetime). Phase 14 adds cleanup.
   682→        const folderIdsToRemove: string[] = [];
   683→
   684→        const collectFolderIds = (folderId: string) => {
   685→          folderIdsToRemove.push(folderId);
   686→          const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   687→          const folder = folders[folderId];
   688→          if (!folder) return;
   689→          for (const child of folder.children) {
   690→            if (child.type === 'folder') {
   691→              collectFolderIds(child.id);
   692→            }
   693→          }
   694→        };
   695→
   696→        for (const item of items) {
   697→          if (item.type === 'folder') {
   698→            collectFolderIds(item.id);
   699→          }
   700→        }
   701→
   702→        // Snapshot the items to be removed before batch delete
   703→        // (needed for addToBin after successful deletion)
   704→        let removedChildren: FolderChild[] = [];
   705→
   706→        const performBatchDelete = async (): Promise<{
   707→          updatedChildren: typeof parentFolder.children;
   708→          newSequenceNumber: bigint;
   709→        }> => {
   710→          const freshParent = getParentFolder();
   711→          if (!freshParent) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   712→
   713→          // Capture removed children before filtering
   714→          removedChildren = freshParent.children.filter((c) => itemIds.has(c.id));
   715→
   716→          // Remove all items from parent's children in one pass
   717→          const updatedChildren = freshParent.children.filter((c) => !itemIds.has(c.id));
   718→
   719→          // Single IPNS publish for the entire batch
   720→          const { newSequenceNumber } = await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   721→            folderId: freshParent.id,
   722→            children: updatedChildren,
   723→            folderKey: freshParent.folderKey,
   724→            ipnsPrivateKey: freshParent.ipnsPrivateKey,
   725→            ipnsName: freshParent.ipnsName,
   726→            sequenceNumber: freshParent.sequenceNumber,
   727→          });
   728→
   729→          return { updatedChildren, newSequenceNumber };
   730→        };
   731→
   732→        const { updatedChildren, newSequenceNumber } = await withConflictRetry(
   733→          performBatchDelete,
   734→          () => resyncFolder(parentFolder.ipnsName, parentFolder.id)
   735→        );
   736→
   737→        // Update local state -- remove all nested folders from store
   738→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   739→        store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   740→        store.updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
   741→        for (const folderId of folderIdsToRemove) {
   742→          store.removeFolder(folderId);
   743→        }
   744→
   745→        // Fire-and-forget: add all deleted items to recycle bin (single IPNS publish)
   746→        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   747→        if (auth.vaultKeypair && removedChildren.length > 0) {
   748→          const parentPath = buildFolderPath(parentId);
   749→          void addManyToBin({
   750→            items: removedChildren,
   751→            parentIpnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   752→            parentPath,
   753→            userPublicKey: auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey,
   754→            userPrivateKey: auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
   755→          }).catch(() => {
   756→            console.error('[Delete] Failed to add batch to bin (non-blocking)');
   757→          });
   758→        }
   759→
   760→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   761→      } catch (err) {
   762→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete items';
   763→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   764→        throw err;
   765→      }
   766→    },
   767→    []
   768→  );
   769→
   770→  return {
   771→    ...state,
   772→    createFolder: handleCreate,
   773→    renameItem: handleRename,
   774→    moveItem: handleMove,
   775→    moveItems: handleMoveItems,
   776→    deleteItem: handleDelete,
   777→    deleteItems: handleDeleteItems,
   778→  };
   779→}
   780→
```

```
     1→/**
     2→ * Centralized cleanup of all user-scoped Zustand stores.
     3→ *
     4→ * Called from:
     5→ * - useAuth logout (normal logout flow)
     6→ * - apiClient 401 interceptor (forced logout on token refresh failure)
     7→ *
     8→ * Order matters: clear stores holding crypto keys BEFORE clearing auth state,
     9→ * so sensitive key material is zeroed from memory first. [SECURITY: HIGH-02]
    10→ */
    11→import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';
    12→import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
    13→import { useSyncStore } from '../stores/sync.store';
    14→import { useDeviceRegistryStore } from '../stores/device-registry.store';
    15→import { useBinStore } from '../stores/bin.store';
    16→import { useShareStore } from '../stores/share.store';
    17→import { useQuotaStore } from '../stores/quota.store';
    18→import { useMfaStore } from '../stores/mfa.store';
    19→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
    20→import { clearFileSizeCache } from '../hooks/useFileSize';
    21→
    22→export function clearAllUserStores(): void {
    23→  // 1. Clear stores with crypto key material first
    24→  useFolderStore.getState().clearFolders();
    25→  useVaultStore.getState().clearVaultKeys();
    26→
    27→  // 2. Clear remaining user-scoped stores
    28→  useSyncStore.getState().reset();
    29→  useDeviceRegistryStore.getState().clearRegistry();
    30→  useBinStore.getState().clearBin();
    31→  useShareStore.getState().clearShares();
    32→  useQuotaStore.getState().reset();
    33→  useMfaStore.getState().reset();
    34→  clearFileSizeCache();
    35→
    36→  // 3. Clear auth state last (zeros keypair memory, sets isAuthenticated=false)
    37→  useAuthStore.getState().logout();
    38→}
    39→
```

```
     1→import { create } from 'zustand';
     2→
     3→/**
     4→ * A received share from another CipherBox user.
     5→ * Contains the ECIES-wrapped item key for decryption.
     6→ */
     7→export type ReceivedShare = {
     8→  shareId: string;
     9→  sharerPublicKey: string;
    10→  itemType: 'folder' | 'file';
    11→  ipnsName: string;
    12→  itemName: string;
    13→  /** Hex-encoded ECIES ciphertext of the item key */
    14→  encryptedKey: string;
    15→  createdAt: string;
    16→};
    17→
    18→/**
    19→ * A share sent by the current user to another CipherBox user.
    20→ * Does not contain the encrypted key (sharer already has access).
    21→ */
    22→export type SentShare = {
    23→  shareId: string;
    24→  recipientPublicKey: string;
    25→  itemType: 'folder' | 'file';
    26→  ipnsName: string;
    27→  itemName: string;
    28→  createdAt: string;
    29→};
    30→
    31→type ShareState = {
    32→  receivedShares: ReceivedShare[];
    33→  sentShares: SentShare[];
    34→  isLoadingReceived: boolean;
    35→  isLoadingSent: boolean;
    36→  lastReceivedFetchedAt: number | null;
    37→  lastSentFetchedAt: number | null;
    38→
    39→  // Actions
    40→  setReceivedShares: (shares: ReceivedShare[]) => void;
    41→  setSentShares: (shares: SentShare[]) => void;
    42→  setLoadingReceived: (loading: boolean) => void;
    43→  setLoadingSent: (loading: boolean) => void;
    44→  addSentShare: (share: SentShare) => void;
    45→  removeSentShare: (shareId: string) => void;
    46→  removeReceivedShare: (shareId: string) => void;
    47→  clearShares: () => void;
    48→};
    49→
    50→/**
    51→ * Share store for managing sent/received share state.
    52→ *
    53→ * Maintains the in-memory lists of shares the user has
    54→ * sent to others and received from others.
    55→ *
    56→ * Call clearShares() on logout to reset state.
    57→ */
    58→export const useShareStore = create<ShareState>((set) => ({
    59→  // State
    60→  receivedShares: [],
    61→  sentShares: [],
    62→  isLoadingReceived: false,
    63→  isLoadingSent: false,
    64→  lastReceivedFetchedAt: null,
    65→  lastSentFetchedAt: null,
    66→
    67→  // Actions
    68→  setReceivedShares: (shares) =>
    69→    set({ receivedShares: shares, isLoadingReceived: false, lastReceivedFetchedAt: Date.now() }),
    70→
    71→  setSentShares: (shares) =>
    72→    set({ sentShares: shares, isLoadingSent: false, lastSentFetchedAt: Date.now() }),
    73→
    74→  setLoadingReceived: (loading) => set({ isLoadingReceived: loading }),
    75→
    76→  setLoadingSent: (loading) => set({ isLoadingSent: loading }),
    77→
    78→  addSentShare: (share) =>
    79→    set((state) => ({
    80→      sentShares: [...state.sentShares, share],
    81→    })),
    82→
    83→  removeSentShare: (shareId) =>
    84→    set((state) => ({
    85→      sentShares: state.sentShares.filter((s) => s.shareId !== shareId),
    86→    })),
    87→
    88→  removeReceivedShare: (shareId) =>
    89→    set((state) => ({
    90→      receivedShares: state.receivedShares.filter((s) => s.shareId !== shareId),
    91→    })),
    92→
    93→  clearShares: () =>
    94→    set({
    95→      receivedShares: [],
    96→      sentShares: [],
    97→      isLoadingReceived: false,
    98→      isLoadingSent: false,
    99→      lastReceivedFetchedAt: null,
   100→      lastSentFetchedAt: null,
   101→    }),
   102→}));
   103→
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/api/src/shares/shares.service.ts",
  "old_string": "  /**\n   * Get all active, non-hidden shares received by the user.\n   * Includes sharer relation for publicKey display.\n   */\n  async getReceivedShares(recipientId: string): Promise<Share[]> {\n    return this.shareRepo.find({\n      where: {\n        recipientId,\n        revokedAt: IsNull(),\n        hiddenByRecipient: false,\n      },\n      relations: ['sharer'],\n      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get all active shares sent by the user.\n   * Includes recipient relation for publicKey display.\n   */\n  async getSentShares(sharerId: string): Promise<Share[]> {\n    return this.shareRepo.find({\n      where: {\n        sharerId,\n        revokedAt: IsNull(),\n      },\n      relations: ['recipient'],\n      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },\n    });\n  }",
  "new_string": "  /**\n   * Get active, non-hidden shares received by the user (paginated).\n   * Includes sharer relation for publicKey display.\n   */\n  async getReceivedShares(\n    recipientId: string,\n    limit: number,\n    offset: number\n  ): Promise<{ shares: Share[]; total: number }> {\n    const [shares, total] = await this.shareRepo.findAndCount({\n      where: {\n        recipientId,\n        revokedAt: IsNull(),\n        hiddenByRecipient: false,\n      },\n      relations: ['sharer'],\n      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },\n      take: limit,\n      skip: offset,\n    });\n    return { shares, total };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get active shares sent by the user (paginated).\n   * Includes recipient relation for publicKey display.\n   */\n  async getSentShares(\n    sharerId: string,\n    limit: number,\n    offset: number\n  ): Promise<{ shares: Share[]; total: number }> {\n    const [shares, total] = await this.shareRepo.findAndCount({\n      where: {\n        sharerId,\n        revokedAt: IsNull(),\n      },\n      relations: ['recipient'],\n      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },\n      take: limit,\n      skip: offset,\n    });\n    return { shares, total };\n  }"
}
```

```json
}
```

```json
  "content": "import { create } from 'zustand';\n\nexport type Notification = {\n  id: string;\n  type: 'info' | 'warning' | 'error';\n  message: string;\n  createdAt: number;\n};\n\ntype NotificationState = {\n  notifications: Notification[];\n  addNotification: (type: Notification['type'], message: string) => void;\n  dismissNotification: (id: string) => void;\n  clearNotifications: () => void;\n};\n\nexport const useNotificationStore = create<NotificationState>((set) => ({\n  notifications: [],\n\n  addNotification: (type, message) =>\n    set((state) => ({\n      notifications: [\n        ...state.notifications,\n        {\n          id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n          type,\n          message,\n          createdAt: Date.now(),\n        },\n      ],\n    })),\n\n  dismissNotification: (id) =>\n    set((state) => ({\n      notifications: state.notifications.filter((n) => n.id !== id),\n    })),\n\n  clearNotifications: () => set({ notifications: [] }),\n}));\n"
}
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "content": "import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\nimport { useNotificationStore, type Notification } from '../stores/notification.store';\n\nconst AUTO_DISMISS_MS = 8000;\n\nconst borderColors: Record<Notification['type'], string> = {\n  info: 'var(--color-green-primary)',\n  warning: 'var(--color-warning)',\n  error: 'var(--color-error)',\n};\n\nconst labelColors: Record<Notification['type'], string> = {\n  info: 'var(--color-green-primary)',\n  warning: 'var(--color-warning)',\n  error: 'var(--color-error)',\n};\n\nconst labels: Record<Notification['type'], string> = {\n  info: '[INFO]',\n  warning: '[WARN]',\n  error: '[ERR]',\n};\n\nexport function NotificationToast() {\n  const notifications = useNotificationStore((s) => s.notifications);\n  const dismissNotification = useNotificationStore((s) => s.dismissNotification);\n  const timersRef = useRef<Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>>(new Map());\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const timers = timersRef.current;\n    for (const n of notifications) {\n      if (!timers.has(n.id)) {\n        const timer = setTimeout(() => {\n          dismissNotification(n.id);\n          timers.delete(n.id);\n        }, AUTO_DISMISS_MS);\n        timers.set(n.id, timer);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Clean up timers for removed notifications\n    for (const [id, timer] of timers) {\n      if (!notifications.some((n) => n.id === id)) {\n        clearTimeout(timer);\n        timers.delete(id);\n      }\n    }\n  }, [notifications, dismissNotification]);\n\n  // Cleanup all timers on unmount\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const timers = timersRef.current;\n    return () => {\n      for (const timer of timers.values()) {\n        clearTimeout(timer);\n      }\n      timers.clear();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  if (notifications.length === 0) return null;\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      style={{\n        position: 'fixed',\n        bottom: 16,\n        right: 16,\n        zIndex: 9999,\n        display: 'flex',\n        flexDirection: 'column',\n        gap: 8,\n        maxWidth: 400,\n        pointerEvents: 'auto',\n      }}\n    >\n      {notifications.map((n) => (\n        <div\n          key={n.id}\n          role=\"status\"\n          style={{\n            background: 'rgb(0 0 0 / 92%)',\n            border: `1px solid ${borderColors[n.type]}`,\n            borderRadius: 2,\n            padding: '8px 12px',\n            fontFamily: 'var(--font-family-mono)',\n            fontSize: 'var(--font-size-sm)',\n            color: 'var(--color-text-primary)',\n            display: 'flex',\n            alignItems: 'flex-start',\n            gap: 8,\n          }}\n        >\n          <span style={{ color: labelColors[n.type], flexShrink: 0 }}>{labels[n.type]}</span>\n          <span style={{ flex: 1 }}>{n.message}</span>\n          <button\n            onClick={() => dismissNotification(n.id)}\n            style={{\n              background: 'none',\n              border: 'none',\n              color: 'var(--color-text-muted)',\n              cursor: 'pointer',\n              padding: 0,\n              fontFamily: 'var(--font-family-mono)',\n              fontSize: 'var(--font-size-sm)',\n              flexShrink: 0,\n            }}\n            aria-label=\"Dismiss notification\"\n          >\n            [x]\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/share.service.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function reWrapForRecipients(params: {\n  folderIpnsName: string;\n  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>;\n  currentFolderId: string | null;\n  newItems: Array<{\n    keyType: 'file' | 'folder';\n    itemId: string;\n    plaintextKey: Uint8Array;\n  }>;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const coveringShares = await findCoveringShares(\n    params.folderIpnsName,\n    params.folders,\n    params.currentFolderId\n  );\n\n  if (coveringShares.length === 0) return;\n\n  // For each share recipient, re-wrap all new item keys\n  for (const share of coveringShares) {\n    try {\n      const recipientPubKey = hexToBytes(\n        share.recipientPublicKey.startsWith('0x')\n          ? share.recipientPublicKey.slice(2)\n          : share.recipientPublicKey\n      );\n\n      const wrappedKeys: Array<{\n        keyType: 'file' | 'folder';\n        itemId: string;\n        encryptedKey: string;\n      }> = [];\n\n      for (const item of params.newItems) {\n        const wrapped = await wrapKey(item.plaintextKey, recipientPubKey);\n        wrappedKeys.push({\n          keyType: item.keyType,\n          itemId: item.itemId,\n          encryptedKey: bytesToHex(wrapped),\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Add the wrapped keys to this share via API\n      await addShareKeys(share.shareId, wrappedKeys);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.warn(\n        `[share] Failed to re-wrap keys for recipient ${share.recipientPublicKey.slice(0, 10)}...:`,\n        err\n      );\n    }\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "export async function reWrapForRecipients(params: {\n  folderIpnsName: string;\n  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>;\n  currentFolderId: string | null;\n  newItems: Array<{\n    keyType: 'file' | 'folder';\n    itemId: string;\n    plaintextKey: Uint8Array;\n  }>;\n}): Promise<{ failedRecipients: string[] }> {\n  const coveringShares = await findCoveringShares(\n    params.folderIpnsName,\n    params.folders,\n    params.currentFolderId\n  );\n\n  if (coveringShares.length === 0) return { failedRecipients: [] };\n\n  const failedRecipients: string[] = [];\n\n  // For each share recipient, re-wrap all new item keys\n  for (const share of coveringShares) {\n    try {\n      const recipientPubKey = hexToBytes(\n        share.recipientPublicKey.startsWith('0x')\n          ? share.recipientPublicKey.slice(2)\n          : share.recipientPublicKey\n      );\n\n      const wrappedKeys: Array<{\n        keyType: 'file' | 'folder';\n        itemId: string;\n        encryptedKey: string;\n      }> = [];\n\n      for (const item of params.newItems) {\n        const wrapped = await wrapKey(item.plaintextKey, recipientPubKey);\n        wrappedKeys.push({\n          keyType: item.keyType,\n          itemId: item.itemId,\n          encryptedKey: bytesToHex(wrapped),\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Add the wrapped keys to this share via API\n      await addShareKeys(share.shareId, wrappedKeys);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.warn(\n        `[share] Failed to re-wrap keys for recipient ${share.recipientPublicKey.slice(0, 10)}...:`,\n        err\n      );\n      failedRecipients.push(share.recipientPublicKey);\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { failedRecipients };\n}"
}
```

```
     3→ *
     4→ * Manages the device's Ed25519 keypair in IndexedDB. This keypair is unique
     5→ * per physical device/browser and cannot be re-derived (unlike the user's
     6→ * vault key which comes from Web3Auth).
     7→ *
     8→ * If IndexedDB is unavailable (incognito, etc.) or the stored keypair is
     9→ * cleared by the browser, a new keypair is generated. This creates a new
    10→ * device entry in the registry (the old one becomes orphaned).
    11→ */
    12→
    13→import { generateDeviceKeypair, deriveDeviceId, type DeviceKeypair } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    14→
    15→const DB_NAME = 'cipherbox-device';
    16→const DB_VERSION = 1;
    17→const STORE_NAME = 'keys';
    18→const KEYPAIR_KEY = 'device-ed25519';
    19→
    20→/**
    21→ * Open the IndexedDB database, creating the object store on upgrade.
    22→ */
    23→function openDB(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
    24→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    25→    const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);
    26→    request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
    27→      request.result.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME);
    28→    };
    29→    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
    30→    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
    31→  });
    32→}
    33→
    34→/**
    35→ * Load device keypair from IndexedDB.
    36→ *
    37→ * Stores keys as plain number arrays because Uint8Array doesn't
    38→ * serialize reliably across all browsers in IndexedDB.
    39→ *
    40→ * @returns Keypair if found, null if not found or IndexedDB unavailable
    41→ */
    42→async function loadDeviceKeypair(): Promise<{
    43→  publicKey: Uint8Array;
    44→  privateKey: Uint8Array;
    45→} | null> {
    46→  try {
    47→    const db = await openDB();
    48→    const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readonly');
    49→    const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    50→
    51→    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    52→      const request = store.get(KEYPAIR_KEY);
    53→      request.onsuccess = () => {
    54→        const val = request.result;
    55→        if (val && val.publicKey && val.privateKey) {
    56→          resolve({
    57→            publicKey: new Uint8Array(val.publicKey),
    58→            privateKey: new Uint8Array(val.privateKey),
    59→          });
    60→        } else {
    61→          resolve(null);
    62→        }
    63→      };
    64→      request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
    65→    });
    66→  } catch {
    67→    // IndexedDB unavailable (incognito, etc.)
    68→    return null;
    69→  }
    70→}
    71→
    72→/**
    73→ * Save device keypair to IndexedDB.
    74→ *
    75→ * Converts Uint8Array to plain number arrays before storing
    76→ * to avoid serialization issues across browsers.
    77→ */
    78→async function saveDeviceKeypair(keypair: {
    79→  publicKey: Uint8Array;
    80→  privateKey: Uint8Array;
    81→}): Promise<void> {
    82→  const db = await openDB();
    83→  const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite');
    84→  const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    85→
    86→  store.put(
    87→    {
    88→      publicKey: Array.from(keypair.publicKey),
    89→      privateKey: Array.from(keypair.privateKey),
    90→    },
    91→    KEYPAIR_KEY
    92→  );
    93→
    94→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    95→    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    96→    tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
    97→  });
    98→}
    99→
   100→/**
   101→ * Get or create the device's Ed25519 identity.
   102→ *
   103→ * Tries to load an existing keypair from IndexedDB first.
   104→ * If not found, generates a new one and persists it.
   105→ *
   106→ * The returned DeviceKeypair includes:
   107→ * - publicKey: 32-byte Ed25519 public key
   108→ * - privateKey: 32-byte Ed25519 private key (seed)
   109→ * - deviceId: SHA-256 hex of the public key
   110→ *
   111→ * @returns Device keypair with derived device ID
   112→ */
   113→export async function getOrCreateDeviceIdentity(): Promise<DeviceKeypair> {
   114→  // Try loading existing keypair from IndexedDB
   115→  const stored = await loadDeviceKeypair();
   116→  if (stored) {
   117→    const deviceId = deriveDeviceId(stored.publicKey);
   118→    return {
   119→      publicKey: stored.publicKey,
   120→      privateKey: stored.privateKey,
   121→      deviceId,
   122→    };
   123→  }
   124→
   125→  // Generate new keypair and persist
   126→  const keypair = generateDeviceKeypair();
   127→  await saveDeviceKeypair({
   128→    publicKey: keypair.publicKey,
   129→    privateKey: keypair.privateKey,
   130→  });
   131→
   132→  return keypair;
   133→}
   134→
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/lib/device/identity.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Device Identity Persistence\n *\n * Manages the device's Ed25519 keypair in IndexedDB. This keypair is unique\n * per physical device/browser and cannot be re-derived (unlike the user's\n * vault key which comes from Web3Auth).\n *\n * [H-08] The device private key is encrypted at rest using AES-256-GCM with\n * a key derived via HKDF from the user's vault private key. This prevents\n * extraction of the device private key if IndexedDB is compromised.\n *\n * If IndexedDB is unavailable (incognito, etc.) or the stored keypair is\n * cleared by the browser, a new keypair is generated. This creates a new\n * device entry in the registry (the old one becomes orphaned).\n *\n * Migration: Old v1 (plaintext) entries lack a `version` field. When\n * `loadDeviceKeypair` encounters a v1 entry, it returns null, triggering\n * new keypair generation. The old device entry becomes orphaned (acceptable\n * per existing design doc).\n */\n\nimport { generateDeviceKeypair, deriveDeviceId, type DeviceKeypair } from '@cipherbox/crypto';\n\nconst DB_NAME = 'cipherbox-device';\nconst DB_VERSION = 1;\nconst STORE_NAME = 'keys';\nconst KEYPAIR_KEY = 'device-ed25519';\nconst HKDF_INFO = 'cipherbox-device-key-wrap-v1';\nconst STORAGE_VERSION = 2;\n\n/**\n * Open the IndexedDB database, creating the object store on upgrade.\n */\nfunction openDB(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);\n    request.onupgradeneeded = () => {\n      request.result.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME);\n    };\n    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);\n    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Derive AES-256-GCM wrapping key from the user's vault private key via HKDF.\n * Same pattern as search-index.service.ts.\n */\nasync function deriveDeviceWrappingKey(vaultPrivateKey: Uint8Array): Promise<CryptoKey> {\n  const baseKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\n    'raw',\n    vaultPrivateKey as BufferSource,\n    'HKDF',\n    false,\n    ['deriveKey']\n  );\n\n  return crypto.subtle.deriveKey(\n    {\n      name: 'HKDF',\n      hash: 'SHA-256',\n      salt: new Uint8Array(0),\n      info: new TextEncoder().encode(HKDF_INFO),\n    },\n    baseKey,\n    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },\n    false,\n    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Load device keypair from IndexedDB.\n *\n * Only loads v2 (encrypted) entries. Returns null for v1 (plaintext legacy)\n * entries, triggering automatic migration via new keypair generation.\n *\n * @param vaultPrivateKey - User's vault private key for HKDF key derivation\n * @returns Keypair if found and decryptable, null otherwise\n */\nasync function loadDeviceKeypair(\n  vaultPrivateKey: Uint8Array\n): Promise<{ publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array } | null> {\n  try {\n    const db = await openDB();\n    const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readonly');\n    const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);\n\n    const val = await new Promise<Record<string, unknown> | undefined>((resolve, reject) => {\n      const request = store.get(KEYPAIR_KEY);\n      request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result as Record<string, unknown> | undefined);\n      request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n    });\n\n    if (!val || !val.publicKey) return null;\n\n    // v1 (plaintext legacy) — discard and regenerate\n    if (val.version !== STORAGE_VERSION) return null;\n\n    // v2 (encrypted) — decrypt the private key\n    const wrappingKey = await deriveDeviceWrappingKey(vaultPrivateKey);\n    const iv = new Uint8Array(val.iv as number[]);\n    const encryptedPrivateKey = new Uint8Array(val.encryptedPrivateKey as number[]);\n\n    const decrypted = await crypto.subtle.decrypt(\n      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv },\n      wrappingKey,\n      encryptedPrivateKey\n    );\n\n    return {\n      publicKey: new Uint8Array(val.publicKey as number[]),\n      privateKey: new Uint8Array(decrypted),\n    };\n  } catch {\n    // IndexedDB unavailable, decryption failed, or corrupt data\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Save device keypair to IndexedDB with AES-256-GCM encryption.\n *\n * Converts Uint8Array to plain number arrays before storing\n * to avoid serialization issues across browsers.\n *\n * @param keypair - Device keypair to store\n * @param vaultPrivateKey - User's vault private key for HKDF key derivation\n */\nasync function saveDeviceKeypair(\n  keypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array },\n  vaultPrivateKey: Uint8Array\n): Promise<void> {\n  const wrappingKey = await deriveDeviceWrappingKey(vaultPrivateKey);\n  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));\n  const encrypted = await crypto.subtle.encrypt(\n    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv },\n    wrappingKey,\n    keypair.privateKey\n  );\n\n  const db = await openDB();\n  const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite');\n  const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);\n\n  store.put(\n    {\n      publicKey: Array.from(keypair.publicKey),\n      encryptedPrivateKey: Array.from(new Uint8Array(encrypted)),\n      iv: Array.from(iv),\n      version: STORAGE_VERSION,\n    },\n    KEYPAIR_KEY\n  );\n\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();\n    tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Get or create the device's Ed25519 identity.\n *\n * Tries to load an existing keypair from IndexedDB first.\n * If not found (or legacy v1 plaintext format), generates a new one\n * and persists it encrypted with the vault private key.\n *\n * The returned DeviceKeypair includes:\n * - publicKey: 32-byte Ed25519 public key\n * - privateKey: 32-byte Ed25519 private key (seed)\n * - deviceId: SHA-256 hex of the public key\n *\n * @param vaultPrivateKey - User's vault private key for encrypting at rest\n * @returns Device keypair with derived device ID\n */\nexport async function getOrCreateDeviceIdentity(\n  vaultPrivateKey: Uint8Array\n): Promise<DeviceKeypair> {\n  // Try loading existing keypair from IndexedDB\n  const stored = await loadDeviceKeypair(vaultPrivateKey);\n  if (stored) {\n    const deviceId = deriveDeviceId(stored.publicKey);\n    return {\n      publicKey: stored.publicKey,\n      privateKey: stored.privateKey,\n      deviceId,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Generate new keypair and persist (encrypted)\n  const keypair = generateDeviceKeypair();\n  await saveDeviceKeypair(\n    { publicKey: keypair.publicKey, privateKey: keypair.privateKey },\n    vaultPrivateKey\n  );\n\n  return keypair;\n}\n"
}
```

> AGENT

The requester call (line 233) only needs `deviceId` which is `SHA-256(publicKey)`. The new device is in REQUIRED_SHARE state — it doesn't have vault keys yet. We need to handle this differently.

Looking at this more carefully: `requestApproval` (line 233) is the NEW device — no vault key available. It only needs `deviceId` from the identity. The approver calls (lines 365, 387) have the user fully logged in.

The approach: make `vaultPrivateKey` optional. When called without it, we can still create/load (but can't decrypt an encrypted key). However, the new device needs to create a device identity to get a `deviceId` for the bulletin board request. On the new device, there's no existing encrypted key (it's a brand-new browser), so we'd generate a new one... but we can't encrypt it without vault key.

Actually, looking at the flow more carefully: the NEW device calls `getOrCreateDeviceIdentity()` during `requestApproval`, before vault keys are available. After the factor key is received and the user completes login, `useAuth` calls `getOrCreateDeviceIdentity(vaultPrivateKey)` again in the device registry init. At that point, the keypair exists in memory from the first call but was saved unencrypted (or not saved at all).

The cleanest approach: store a temporary unencrypted identity during `requestApproval` (since we have no vault key), then re-encrypt it later when useAuth initializes with vault keys. But that adds complexity.

Simpler: during `requestApproval`, we only need `deviceId`. We can generate a temporary keypair that we don't persist. OR: make the function accept `undefined` for vaultPrivateKey and store plaintext if no key available (which will be migrated on next login).

Actually, the simplest and most secure approach: during `requestApproval`, generate a temporary keypair for the deviceId (don't persist), and the real persistence happens in `useAuth` after login when vault keys are available. Let me check if the deviceId from the request matters beyond identifying the device...

Looking at the code flow: `requestApproval` → uses `deviceIdentity.deviceId` for the bulletin board request. After approval completes → `useAuth.initializeOrLoadVault` → calls `getOrCreateDeviceIdentity(vaultPrivateKey)` which generates a new keypair (since the old encrypted one can't be loaded). So the device might get a different deviceId.

But actually, the deviceId in the approval request is just for display/tracking. It doesn't need to match the one registered in the device registry. The approval flow is about the ephemeral key for ECIES, not the device identity.

So the simplest fix: for `requestApproval`, just generate a temporary keypair for the deviceId without persisting. For the approver calls, pass the vault private key.

Actually, even simpler — let me re-read how `requestApproval` uses deviceId:

It calls `deviceApprovalApi.createRequest({ deviceId, deviceName, ephemeralPublicKey })`. The deviceId and deviceName are just metadata for the approver to see which device is requesting. They don't need to be stable across sessions.

Let me make `vaultPrivateKey` optional. If not provided, we generate a new keypair and don't persist (for the requester scenario). If provided, we try to load encrypted, and save encrypted.

Returns null for v1 (plaintext legacy)\n * entries, triggering automatic migration via new keypair generation.\n *\n * @param vaultPrivateKey - User's vault private key for HKDF key derivation\n * @returns Keypair if found and decryptable, null otherwise\n */\nasync function loadDeviceKeypair(\n  vaultPrivateKey: Uint8Array\n): Promise<{ publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array } | null> {\n  try {\n    const db = await openDB();\n    const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readonly');\n    const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);\n\n    const val = await new Promise<Record<string, unknown> | undefined>((resolve, reject) => {\n      const request = store.get(KEYPAIR_KEY);\n      request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result as Record<string, unknown> | undefined);\n      request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);\n    });\n\n    if (!val || !val.publicKey) return null;\n\n    // v1 (plaintext legacy) — discard and regenerate\n    if (val.version !== STORAGE_VERSION) return null;\n\n    // v2 (encrypted) — decrypt the private key\n    const wrappingKey = await deriveDeviceWrappingKey(vaultPrivateKey);\n    const iv = new Uint8Array(val.iv as number[]);\n    const encryptedPrivateKey = new Uint8Array(val.encryptedPrivateKey as number[]);\n\n    const decrypted = await crypto.subtle.decrypt(\n      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv },\n      wrappingKey,\n      encryptedPrivateKey\n    );\n\n    return {\n      publicKey: new Uint8Array(val.publicKey as number[]),\n      privateKey: new Uint8Array(decrypted),\n    };\n  } catch {\n    // IndexedDB unavailable, decryption failed, or corrupt data\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Save device keypair to IndexedDB with AES-256-GCM encryption.\n *\n * Converts Uint8Array to plain number arrays before storing\n * to avoid serialization issues across browsers.\n *\n * @param keypair - Device keypair to store\n * @param vaultPrivateKey - User's vault private key for HKDF key derivation\n */\nasync function saveDeviceKeypair(\n  keypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array },\n  vaultPrivateKey: Uint8Array\n): Promise<void> {\n  const wrappingKey = await deriveDeviceWrappingKey(vaultPrivateKey);\n  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));\n  const encrypted = await crypto.subtle.encrypt(\n    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv },\n    wrappingKey,\n    keypair.privateKey\n  );\n\n  const db = await openDB();\n  const tx = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite');\n  const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);\n\n  store.put(\n    {\n      publicKey: Array.from(keypair.publicKey),\n      encryptedPrivateKey: Array.from(new Uint8Array(encrypted)),\n      iv: Array.from(iv),\n      version: STORAGE_VERSION,\n    },\n    KEYPAIR_KEY\n  );\n\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();\n    tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Get or create the device's Ed25519 identity.\n *\n * Tries to load an existing keypair from IndexedDB first.\n * If not found (or legacy v1 plaintext format), generates a new one\n * and persists it encrypted with the vault private key.\n *\n * The returned DeviceKeypair includes:\n * - publicKey: 32-byte Ed25519 public key\n * - privateKey: 32-byte Ed25519 private key (seed)\n * - deviceId: SHA-256 hex of the public key\n *\n * @param vaultPrivateKey - User's vault private key for encrypting at rest.\n *   When undefined (e.g., during REQUIRED_SHARE state before vault is available),\n *   generates a temporary keypair without persisting. The real identity is\n *   created/loaded when useAuth calls this again after login with the vault key.\n * @returns Device keypair with derived device ID\n */\nexport async function getOrCreateDeviceIdentity(\n  vaultPrivateKey?: Uint8Array\n): Promise<DeviceKeypair> {\n  // Without vault key, return ephemeral identity (not persisted)\n  if (!vaultPrivateKey) {\n    const keypair = generateDeviceKeypair();\n    return keypair;\n  }\n\n  // Try loading existing keypair from IndexedDB\n  const stored = await loadDeviceKeypair(vaultPrivateKey);\n  if (stored) {\n    const deviceId = deriveDeviceId(stored.publicKey);\n    return {\n      publicKey: stored.publicKey,\n      privateKey: stored.privateKey,\n      deviceId,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Generate new keypair and persist (encrypted)\n  const keypair = generateDeviceKeypair();\n  await saveDeviceKeypair(\n    { publicKey: keypair.publicKey, privateKey: keypair.privateKey },\n    vaultPrivateKey\n  );\n\n  return keypair;\n}\n"
```json
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/share.service.ts",
  "old_string": "/**\n * Fetch all active, non-hidden shares received by the current user.\n * Updates the share store with the results.\n */\nexport async function fetchReceivedShares(): Promise<ReceivedShare[]> {\n  const response = await sharesControllerGetReceivedShares();\n\n  return response.map((s) => ({\n    shareId: s.shareId,\n    sharerPublicKey: s.sharerPublicKey,\n    itemType: s.itemType as 'folder' | 'file',\n    ipnsName: s.ipnsName,\n    itemName: s.itemName,\n    encryptedKey: s.encryptedKey,\n    createdAt: String(s.createdAt),\n  }));\n}\n\n/**\n * Fetch all active shares sent by the current user.\n * Updates the share store with the results.\n */\nexport async function fetchSentShares(): Promise<SentShare[]> {\n  const response = await sharesControllerGetSentShares();\n\n  return response.map((s) => ({\n    shareId: s.shareId,\n    recipientPublicKey: s.recipientPublicKey,\n    itemType: s.itemType as 'folder' | 'file',\n    ipnsName: s.ipnsName,\n    itemName: s.itemName,\n    createdAt: String(s.createdAt),\n  }));\n}",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Fetch active, non-hidden shares received by the current user (paginated).\n */\nexport async function fetchReceivedShares(\n  limit = 50,\n  offset = 0\n): Promise<{ shares: ReceivedShare[]; total: number }> {\n  const response = await sharesControllerGetReceivedShares({ limit, offset });\n\n  return {\n    shares: response.shares.map((s) => ({\n      shareId: s.shareId,\n      sharerPublicKey: s.sharerPublicKey,\n      itemType: s.itemType as 'folder' | 'file',\n      ipnsName: s.ipnsName,\n      itemName: s.itemName,\n      encryptedKey: s.encryptedKey,\n      createdAt: String(s.createdAt),\n    })),\n    total: response.total,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Fetch active shares sent by the current user (paginated).\n */\nexport async function fetchSentShares(\n  limit = 50,\n  offset = 0\n): Promise<{ shares: SentShare[]; total: number }> {\n  const response = await sharesControllerGetSentShares({ limit, offset });\n\n  return {\n    shares: response.shares.map((s) => ({\n      shareId: s.shareId,\n      recipientPublicKey: s.recipientPublicKey,\n      itemType: s.itemType as 'folder' | 'file',\n      ipnsName: s.ipnsName,\n      itemName: s.itemName,\n      createdAt: String(s.createdAt),\n    })),\n    total: response.total,\n  };\n}"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/share.service.ts",
  "old_string": "/**\n * Check if a CipherBox user exists with the given secp256k1 public key.\n * Returns false if no user is found (404).\n *\n * @param publicKeyHex - Uncompressed secp256k1 public key (0x04... format)\n */\nexport async function lookupUser(publicKeyHex: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  try {\n    await sharesControllerLookupUser({ publicKey: publicKeyHex });\n    return true;\n  } catch (err: unknown) {\n    // Only treat 404 as \"user not found\"; re-throw network/server errors\n    if (\n      err &&\n      typeof err === 'object' &&\n      'status' in err &&\n      (err as { status: number }).status === 404\n    ) {\n      return false;\n    }\n    throw err;\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Check if a CipherBox user exists with the given secp256k1 public key.\n *\n * @param publicKeyHex - Uncompressed secp256k1 public key (0x04... format)\n */\nexport async function lookupUser(publicKeyHex: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  const result = await sharesControllerLookupUser({ publicKey: publicKeyHex });\n  return result.exists;\n}"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/share.service.ts",
  "old_string": "async function ensureFreshSentShares(): Promise<SentShare[]> {\n  const store = useShareStore.getState();\n  if (store.lastSentFetchedAt && Date.now() - store.lastSentFetchedAt < 30_000) {\n    return store.sentShares;\n  }\n  const shares = await fetchSentShares();\n  useShareStore.getState().setSentShares(shares);\n  return shares;\n}",
  "new_string": "async function ensureFreshSentShares(): Promise<SentShare[]> {\n  const store = useShareStore.getState();\n  if (store.lastSentFetchedAt && Date.now() - store.lastSentFetchedAt < 30_000) {\n    return store.sentShares;\n  }\n  // Use high limit to fetch all shares — re-wrapping needs the full set\n  const { shares } = await fetchSentShares(1000);\n  useShareStore.getState().setSentShares(shares);\n  return shares;\n}"
}
```

```
     1→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
     2→
     3→// Use VITE_API_URL if set, otherwise default to same-origin /api path for deployed environments
     4→const BASE_URL =
     5→  import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL ||
     6→  (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? `${window.location.origin}/api` : '/api');
     7→
     8→export const customInstance = async <T>(config: {
     9→  url: string;
    10→  method: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'DELETE' | 'PATCH';
    11→  params?: Record<string, string>;
    12→  data?: unknown;
    13→  headers?: Record<string, string>;
    14→  signal?: AbortSignal;
    15→  responseType?: 'json' | 'blob' | 'text';
    16→}): Promise<T> => {
    17→  const { url, method, params, data, headers, signal, responseType = 'json' } = config;
    18→
    19→  // Get auth token from store
    20→  const accessToken = useAuthStore.getState().accessToken;
    21→
    22→  const queryString = params ? '?' + new URLSearchParams(params).toString() : '';
    23→
    24→  const response = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}${url}${queryString}`, {
    25→    method,
    26→    headers: {
    27→      // Only set Content-Type for JSON requests with body
    28→      ...(data && responseType === 'json' ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}),
    29→      ...(accessToken ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` } : {}),
    30→      ...headers,
    31→    },
    32→    body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    33→    signal,
    34→  });
    35→
    36→  if (!response.ok) {
    37→    const err = new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
    38→    (err as Error & { status: number }).status = response.status;
    39→    throw err;
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  // Return appropriate response type
    43→  if (responseType === 'blob') {
    44→    return response.blob() as Promise<T>;
    45→  } else if (responseType === 'text') {
    46→    return response.text() as Promise<T>;
    47→  }
    48→  // Responses with no body (204 No Content, 201 with void return, etc.)
    49→  // Read as text first to avoid JSON parse error on empty bodies
    50→  const text = await response.text();
    51→  if (!text.trim()) {
    52→    return undefined as T;
    53→  }
    54→  return JSON.parse(text);
    55→};
    56→
    57→export default customInstance;
    58→
```
